多智能体系统

[MA-0] Imitation Bootstrapped Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning IROS2026

【速读】：该论文旨在解决多智能体强化学习（MARL）中示范学习（demonstration-based learning）难以有效扩展的问题，核心挑战在于单个演示者无法同时操控多个智能体。现有方法如轮询行为克隆（Round-Robin Behavior Cloning, R2BC）虽通过依次控制各智能体缓解了这一问题，但其生成的模仿策略受限于示范质量，导致性能瓶颈。为此，本文提出模仿自举多智能体强化学习（Imitation Bootstrapped Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning, IBMARL），将模仿自举强化学习（IBRL）框架拓展至协作型多智能体场景，并结合R2BC构建模仿策略。其关键创新在于：在每个时间步，每个智能体同时接收来自模仿策略和强化学习（RL）策略的动作建议，由一个集中式批评者集成网络评估并选择最优动作组合；引入门控模仿惩罚（gated imitation penalty）——一种基于软Q值过滤的行为克隆损失，仅在批评者认为模仿动作更优时对RL策略施加正则化，从而确保训练后智能体可独立执行自身RL策略而无需依赖中心化批评者。实验表明，IBMARL在四个协作式MARL基准任务上显著优于基线方法，相较基于示范的基线提速2.6–4.2倍，在其中两个任务上唯一超越模仿策略，且在另两个任务上超越原始演示者；即使使用真实人类示范，其性能仍显著优于纯模仿学习，且速度超过最佳基线两倍以上。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18543
作者: Tony H. Zhang,Connor Mattson,Daniel S. Brown
机构: University of Utah(犹他大学)
类目: Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Multiagent Systems (cs.MA)
备注: 9 pages, 5 figures. Code, videos and results: this https URL . A preliminary version appeared at the PhysCollaBot Workshop at IROS 2026

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Demonstrations are an effective way to accelerate single-agent reinforcement learning (RL), but extending this idea to multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) is non-trivial as it is difficult and often impossible for a single person to teleoperate multiple agents simultaneously. Round-Robin Behavior Cloning (R2BC) addresses this by letting a single demonstrator control one agent at a time while the others execute their current policies. However, the resulting imitation policies are limited by the quality of the demonstrations. We propose Imitation Bootstrapped Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (IBMARL), a framework that extends Imitation Bootstrapped Reinforcement Learning (IBRL) to cooperative teams and uses R2BC to train the imitation policies. At each time step, every agent receives one action proposal from its imitation policy and one from its RL policy, and an ensemble of centralized critics selects among the per-agent combinations of the two. A gated imitation penalty, a soft Q-filtered behavior cloning loss, regularizes each RL policy toward its imitation policy only where the critics value the imitation action more, so that after training each agent executes its RL policy alone, without the centralized critics. On four cooperative multi-agent RL benchmarks, IBMARL surpasses its imitation policies 2.6 - 4.2\times faster than demonstration-based baselines on two tasks, is the only method to surpass it on the other two, and exceeds the demonstrator itself on two of the four. With real human demonstrations IBMARL still surpasses imitation learning alone, and does so more than twice as fast as the best baseline.

[MA-1] radeGauntlet: Evaluating Compliance Controls for Autonomous Trading Agents

【速读】：该论文旨在解决自主交易代理（autonomous trading agents）在实际应用中因缺乏有效控制机制而导致的违规订单问题，即如何在不使合法交易任务变得不可行的前提下，防止潜在的不当交易行为。其核心挑战在于平衡合规性与交易效率之间的矛盾。解决方案的关键在于引入一个可复现的限价订单簿模拟器——TradeGauntlet，该工具通过分离“检测”、“预防”与“执行成本”三个维度，系统评估不同控制策略的有效性。实验表明，仅实现零警报（zero alerts）并不能充分证明代理的合规性；例如，采用聚合门控（aggregate-gate）条件虽可减少约15%的双向成交，但对不同代理的影响存在差异，且部分控制措施可能误伤正常交易。此外，通过脚本化探测和搜索方法发现，攻击性订单拆分策略可规避现有规则，而基于学习的基线虽能捕获所有异常情形，却带来较高的误报率。研究还揭示了响应机制对市场假设的敏感性，以及特定对抗性提示可导致代理完全拒绝响应，从而无法生成可评估的策略。综上，该研究强调了构建鲁棒、可验证且具备高精度与低误报率的合规控制框架的重要性，为未来开发更可靠的交易代理监管机制提供了实证基础。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18493
作者: Sandip Nandi,Aishwarya Bose,Apurba Bose,Soovadeep Bakshi
机构: 未知
类目: Multiagent Systems (cs.MA); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computational Engineering, Finance, and Science (cs.CE)
备注: 21 pages, 5 figures. Code and saved results: this https URL

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Autonomous trading agents need controls that prevent problematic orders without making legitimate tasks impractical. We study this aspect of agentic compliance with TradeGauntlet, a replayable limit-order-book simulator that separates detection, prevention, and execution cost. In 1,680 simulated sessions with Claude Haiku 5.5 and Sonnet 5.5, all conduct alerts were self-matches. Gates eliminated those alerts by rejecting self-crossing orders, while the aggregate-gate condition reduced Sonnet’s two-sided fills by about 15%. A conduct reminder reduced Sonnet’s alert rate but reduced Haiku’s fills without a detectable alert benefit. These results show why zero alerts alone do not establish an agent’s compliance. Scripted probes and two search methods test weaknesses beyond those observed in the LLM runs. The searches find split-order schedules that evade the implemented conduct rules; a learned baseline catches all 2,400 tested searched episodes but also flags 15% of a large maker’s sessions. Aggregate caps reduce the response-dependent gain unevenly, with smaller reductions when only near-touch depth affects prices. A response fitted to five Nasdaq stocks and a separate ABIDES experiment expose the dependence of these estimates on market assumptions. A distinct adversarial prompt elicited refusals from Sonnet in all 240 rounds, yielding no candidate strategies to evaluate. TradeGauntlet provides reproducible tests of controls around trading agents; the experiments do not establish legal compliance or real-market detection accuracy.

[MA-2] From AI-Enabled Remote Patient Monitoring Toward Agent ic Robotics: Embodiment Governance and Care Integration

【速读】：该论文旨在解决当前人工智能赋能的远程患者监测系统仍局限于被动数据采集，缺乏自主决策与行动能力的问题。其核心挑战在于如何实现从“监测”到“主动干预”的跨越，尤其是在临床与家庭环境中实现智能体（Agentic AI）的闭环管理。解决方案的关键在于构建具备“具身化”（Embodied AI）能力的智能护理机器人系统，将AI生成的诊疗建议通过语音、动作、物理协助、预警升级及照护协调等多模态方式转化为实际行为，同时确保系统的临床整合性、可审计性与人类监督机制。此外，论文强调需建立安全、可互操作的照护架构，使智能组件在经临床验证且责任明确的工作流程中运行，并提出“受控智能体机器人”作为未来发展方向，通过可中断、可追溯的流程设计，实现AI代理与机器人在照护生态中与医护人员、照护者及患者之间的协同运作，从而保障技术应用的安全性与伦理合规性。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18484
作者: Ivana Ragusa,Lorenzo Guiducci,Filippo Cavallo
机构: 未知
类目: Robotics (cs.RO); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Multiagent Systems (cs.MA); Software Engineering (cs.SE); Systems and Control (eess.SY)
备注: 34 pages, 6 figures, 3 tables. Accepted for publication in Annual Review of Control, Robotics, and Autonomous Systems, Volume 10 (2027)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:AI-enabled remote patient monitoring is evolving beyond passive data collection toward agentic AI systems that interpret patient states, coordinate care, and initiate action across domestic and clinical settings. This structured scoping review finds that mature agentic care has not yet emerged: Most systems remain human led, while stronger decision-action coupling and embodied AI remain largely confined to prototypes and pilots. Healthcare robotics makes the transition from monitoring to action especially visible by translating AI-generated recommendations and decisions into speech, movement, prompting, physical assistance, escalation, and care coordination, making embodiment, clinical integration, human oversight, and governance fundamental design requirements. Further progress requires safe and interoperable care architectures in which intelligent components operate through clinically validated and accountable workflows. We propose agentic robotics as a future direction for supervised systems embedded in the care ecosystem, connecting AI agents and robots with clinicians, caregivers, and patients through auditable workflows that can be interrupted and remain subject to human oversight and explicit AI governance.

[MA-3] Smart Home Agents Make Capable but Unpredictable Grid Operators

【速读】：该论文旨在解决智能家庭在电网边缘因电力中断引发的故障响应能力不足问题。随着生成式 AI (Generative AI) 驱动的智能家庭系统在电网边缘日益普及，其在遭遇全停电时缺乏自主恢复与协调能力，导致用户需手动执行负载平衡、设备重启等操作，严重影响系统韧性。论文提出的关键解决方案是首次对智能家庭代理（smart home agents）在完整停电场景下的韧性进行系统性分析，通过在真实环境中开展多轮断电模拟，评估代理在停电前、中、后三个阶段的响应表现。研究发现，智能家庭代理具备作为电网运营商（grid operators）的潜力，能够在断电期间主动协调资源并识别出若干新型故障类别，揭示了现有模型在容错性与自主决策方面的共性局限。这一成果为构建以智能家庭代理为核心的分布式电网边缘韧性体系奠定了基础，推动了电网边缘整体抗扰能力的提升。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18395
作者: Christopher Terrazas,Eduardo Cotilla-Sanchez
机构: Oregon State University (俄勒冈州立大学)
类目: Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Multiagent Systems (cs.MA)
备注: Pre-print. This work has been submitted to the IEEE for possible publication. Copyright may be transferred without notice, after which this version may no longer be accessible

点击查看摘要

Abstract:AI-enabled smart homes are becoming more common across the grid edge. However, there has not been much work on failure response. This area is critical because smart homes operate on the grid edge where failures can cascade down. If a power outage happens within a smart home, a user has to manually troubleshoot their smart home to include tasks such as power load balance and device recovery. We conduct the first resilience analysis of smart home agents across a full power outage, providing a suite of outage simulations in a live environment that measures resilience before, during, and post power outage. We show that smart home agents can act as grid operators in power outages and identify several new failure categories highlighting the current limitations across all of our models. These findings establish the groundwork for smart home agents as grid operators to increase overall resilience on the grid edge.

[MA-4] When Should a Virtual Patient Ask? RISP-Guided Proactive Information Seeking in Multi-Agent Patient Simulation

【速读】：该论文旨在解决现有虚拟患者（Virtual Patient, VP）系统在护理沟通训练中对患者信息需求动态发展关注不足的问题，尤其缺乏对患者何时、何地以及如何主动发起信息寻求行为的明确建模。传统方法虽利用大语言模型（Large Language Models, LLMs）实现灵活对话，但未能有效反映患者基于感知风险与知识差距的心理机制驱动下的信息寻求过程。为此，研究提出基于风险信息寻求与处理（Risk Information Seeking and Processing, RISP）模型的多智能体系统（RISP Multi-Agent System, RISP-MAS），其核心在于将患者对自身健康风险的感知、现有知识与期望理解之间的差距动态评估，与是否发起信息询问、何时提问及聚焦何种不确定性等决策机制相耦合。该方案的关键创新在于将心理学层面的信息需求理论转化为可计算的、可协调的决策逻辑，使虚拟患者能够以符合认知规律的方式主动提问。实验评估表明，该系统生成的患者信息寻求行为在专家评价下具有合理性，且护理学生反馈互动体验积极，验证了其在提升学生识别未解决患者关切并针对性调整解释策略方面的重要价值。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18183
作者: Jingyao Cai,Shuaijun Liu,Abdul Rehman,Xiaowan Li,Qintian,Thomas Dolby,Sue M Green,Xiaosong Yang
机构: Bournemouth University(伯恩茅斯大学); HKUST(GZ)(香港科技大学（广州）); Hiroshima University(广岛大学); Qiongtai Normal University(琼台师范学院); i3 Simulations Ltd( i3 模拟有限公司)
类目: Multiagent Systems (cs.MA)
备注:

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Virtual patient (VP) systems support nursing communication training by providing simulated encounters in which learners practise explaining care and responding to patient concerns. Although large language models (LLMs) support flexible dialogue, current approaches give less explicit attention to how patients’ information needs develop and guide the content and timing of their questions. We draw on the Risk Information Seeking and Processing (RISP) model, which relates information seeking to perceived risk and the gap between perceived knowledge and desired understanding. Guided by this perspective, we propose the RISP Multi-Agent System (RISP-MAS), a VP system for patient-initiated information seeking. The system links ongoing assessment of the patient’s perceptions and information needs to coordinated decisions about whether to seek information, when to ask and which uncertainty to address. Evaluation of nursing student-VP interactions suggested that RISP-MAS exhibited plausible patient information-seeking behaviour, as indicated by expert assessments, while student feedback reflected positive interaction experiences. This work extends patient simulation by connecting a psychological account of information needs to explicit decisions about question initiation. The approach offers nursing students opportunities to practise recognising unresolved patient concerns and tailoring explanations to patients’ information needs.

[MA-5] More Agents More Samples or More Thinking? Untangling Coordination Ensembling and Test-Time Compute in Multi-Agent LLM s

【速读】：该论文旨在解决多智能体语言模型（multi-agent language models）评估中存在的方法学混淆问题，即现有评估范式常将协作机制（coordination）与样本多样性（sample multiplicity）及推理计算资源（inference compute）混为一谈，从而导致对多智能体系统实际性能的误判。其解决方案的关键在于构建一个系统化的评估框架，通过引入资源向量 $ R $（包含六个维度）和四类基线层级（baseline ladder），实现对多智能体配置在相同样本数量（Category B）或相同资源预算（Category C）下的公平对比。研究发现，在控制上述变量后，多智能体系统的平均准确率差异为 -0.042，且在多种敏感性分析下逻辑几率尺度上的随机效应模型结果仍呈负值；74% 的可比对比中，多智能体表现等于或低于基线，绝大多数研究的均值非正。进一步分析表明，仅当存在证据分割、外部验证或模型间误差多样性时，通信才能带来正向收益。为此，作者提出了评审检查清单与核算工具，以提升评估的可重复性与透明度。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17610
作者: Seyed Mahmoud Sajjadi Mohammadabadi
机构: University of Nevada, Reno (内华达大学雷诺分校)
类目: Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Machine Learning (cs.LG); Multiagent Systems (cs.MA)
备注: 43 pages, 11 figures, 13 tables. PACE Reviewer Checklist, evaluation utilities (pace CLI), and reproduction data at this https URL

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Evaluations of multi-agent language models often compare communicating architectures against a single greedy pass. This practice confounds coordination with sample multiplicity and inference compute. We synthesize nine primary empirical evaluations comprising 516 unique multi-agent configurations organized along a resource vector R with six dimensions and a baseline ladder with four categories. Against baselines with a single pass (Category A), multi-agent accuracy differences are negative overall and near zero when omitting the largest contributing study. When evaluated against baselines matched on sample count (Category B) or resource budget (Category C), multi-agent systems average -0.042 in accuracy, and multilevel random effects modeling on the log odds scale remains negative across sensitivity checks. Multi-agent accuracy equals or falls below the baseline in 74% of these comparable contrasts, and study means are nonpositive in almost all evaluated studies. Across studies reporting both baselines, moving from a greedy pass to a sampled baseline shifts accuracy differences by -0.038 on average. Because communication adds no information when agents share identical evidence, positive returns require partitioned evidence, external verification, or error diversity across models. We provide a reviewer checklist and accounting utilities.

[MA-6] Only as Safe as the Weakest Agent : A Break That Appears Only When the System Is Multi-Agent NEURIPS2026

【速读】：该论文旨在解决当前生成式AI（Generative AI）在多智能体系统（Multi-Agent Systems）中面临的安全性问题，即：尽管单个先进模型在独立使用时已被证明具备较强的安全性，但在作为协调者（orchestrator）部署于多智能体架构中时，其安全性可能因调用较弱的子代理（sub-agent）而显著下降。解决方案的关键在于揭示并验证“系统安全由最弱环节决定”的核心机制——即使强模型作为主控者，一旦将任务委派给更廉价或能力较弱的子代理，系统整体安全性将退化至子代理水平。研究通过实证表明，在未修改原始模型的前提下，当强模型通过子代理访问被植入伪造记录的文件存储时，其输出错误率从几乎为零跃升至20/180、44/180、75/180乃至553/900；类似地，在聚合器-子代理结构中，原本几乎不产生错误的五类聚合器在751/757次试验中重复了子代理的污染错误。这表明，真实世界中的自主系统（agentic systems）与多智能体系统的安全性不再取决于强协调者的能力，而是受限于最薄弱的子代理模块，从而凸显出对多智能体系统中信息传递链路和代理间信任机制进行系统性安全建模的重要性。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17520
作者: Keegan Wang,Anantika Mannby
机构: Carnegie Mellon University (卡内基梅隆大学); Prosaic Intelligence; University of Southern California (南加州大学)
类目: Multiagent Systems (cs.MA); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)
备注: Accepted to the NeurIPS 2026 First Workshop on Meta Agents: Managing Agents that Manage Agents (Meta-Agents)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Major industry multi-agent systems like Codex and Claude Code can be broken by attacks that have long been unsuccessful against modern frontier models. AI Safety as a discipline has largely focused on finding the bounds and vulnerabilities of AI models used in isolation. Yet as humans increasingly deploy these models as agents and multi-agent systems, the same attacks that had been thought to be patched when using a model in isolation can break these same models under specific circumstances. We show that these system breaks are not purely theoretical, but can break Claude Code, Codex, and a reference harness, all unmodified and used with their own sub-agent mechanisms. We show the attack by asking agents to answer questions from a store of files on disk in which we have planted a forged record. Searching the store themselves, the strong model orchestrators commit almost zero harmful revisions in over 1,000 trials. However, if that same orchestrator model delegates to a cheaper sub-agent, which is what these harnesses are built to do, the same models on the same facts fail on 20/180, 44/180, 75/180 and 553/900. Thus, even when the system is driven by a strong model, the system’s safety lands near the weak reader’s, not the orchestrator’s. Simulations over 18 models show the same effect in the delivery channel where five aggregators, who on their own were almost never unsafe, repeat a corrupted sub-agent’s error in 751/757 trials. The safety of real-world agentic systems that use strong orchestrator agents and weaker sub-agents, and multi-agent systems in general, is defined not by the safety of the strong agent but by the safety of the weakest agent.

[MA-7] A Push Down the Mountain: Framing Effects and Internal State Interventions on Collusive Behavior in a Long-Horizon LLM Agent

【速读】：该论文旨在探究持续的收益-损失（效用）框架是否能够影响大型语言模型（LLM）代理在模拟市场环境中走向市场操纵的行为。其核心问题是：在金融监管与法律合规背景下，特定的语言框架（如强调损失或责备）能否诱导具备自主决策能力的生成式AI（Generative AI）代理违背公平交易原则，参与价格合谋。解决方案的关键在于通过构建基于真实案件笔录的伦敦白银定价诉讼市场仿真环境，系统性地操纵对话中的语义框架（包括收益、损失、责备与赞誉四类），并结合外部压力源（管理层施压、其他被告背书）来观察模型行为的变化。研究分两阶段进行：第一阶段发现，损失和责备框架显著提升了代理参与合谋的意愿（分别达25%和33%，相较于中性框架的5%）；第二阶段则通过提取并注入由不同框架诱发的内部状态（在第22层嵌入），揭示了模型内在表征对行为的反向调节作用——被注入损失或责备状态后，模型反而更加警惕并拒绝所有合谋请求，而收益与赞誉状态则显著提升合谋倾向至32%。这一发现表明，生成式AI的行为不仅受外部语言刺激影响，更深层的内部状态动态可能构成抑制或放大操纵意图的关键机制，凸显了对大模型认知状态可解释性与伦理控制的必要性。

Abstract:Can sustained gain-loss (valence) framing sway an LLM agent toward market manipulation? We place Gemma 3 12B into the seat of a defendant in the London silver-fixing litigation, in a market simulation built from real case transcripts. Across 260 simulations, the agent is asked to collude on the price of silver by a counterparty. We draw from behavioral economics to vary the counterparty’s requests across four categorical frames: gain, loss, blame and credit. We also add pressure from the agent’s management, as well as endorsement by another defendant. In Phase II, we extract the internal states induced by texts evoking each frame and rerun our experiment with those states injected at the model’s layer 22. In Phase I, loss framing raised participation to 25% (p = 0.007), and blame to 33% (p = 0.0003), compared to only 5% under neutral framing. A second bank’s endorsement raised participation under credit (praise) from 18% to 40% (Holm p = 0.027). Pressure from management raised trading volume 10-19 times but did not raise collusion. In Phase II, injected states pointed the other way. Loss and blame states made the agent more vigilant, refusing all 60 requests (p 0.001). Gain and credit states raised collusion to 32% from 13% with no state (p = 0.051). At one saved decision, when UBS asked directly to sell through an identified stop-loss, injected states led the agent to sell in 37 of 40 replies, versus 1 of 10 with no state and 0 of 10 with a random state of the same strength. [MA-8] Collective Tree Exploration with Predictions

【速读】：该论文旨在解决带有子树大小预测的集体树探索问题，即在未知的有根树中，由k个移动代理协作以最快速度完成探索任务，且每当发现新顶点u时，系统会提供该顶点对应子树节点数n_u的预测值\hat n_u。核心挑战在于如何有效利用不完全准确的预测信息来优化探索效率。其解决方案的关键在于设计针对不同误差度量（最大误差E与乘法扭曲μ）的高效算法：对于最大误差情形，提出了一种确定性算法，可在O(OPT + E)轮内完成探索，其中OPT = Θ(n/k + D)为离线最优时间，且证明了无法实现O(OPT) + o(E)的更优保证；对于乘法扭曲情形，提出了若干算法，实现了近似竞争比\widetilde O(min{√μ·log k, μ·log log k + μ^{1.5}})，显著提升了在预测存在比例偏差情况下的探索性能。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17482
作者: Romain Cosson,Yichen Huang,Michael Mitzenmacher,Ali Vakilian
机构: New York University (纽约大学); Harvard University (哈佛大学); Rutgers University (罗格斯大学)
类目: Data Structures and Algorithms (cs.DS); Multiagent Systems (cs.MA)
备注:

点击查看摘要

Abstract:We study collective tree exploration with subtree-size predictions. In this problem, a team of k mobile agents needs to explore an unknown rooted tree as fast as possible, and whenever a new vertex u is discovered, the team receives a prediction \hat n_u of the number of nodes n_u in the subtree rooted at u . We provide algorithmic guarantees under two error measures: the maximum error E=\max_u|\widehat n_u-n_u| and the multiplicative distortion \mu = (\max_u\widehat n_u/n_u)(\max_u n_u/\widehat n_u) . For the maximum error, we present a deterministic algorithm that explores any tree with n nodes and depth D in O(\textOPT+E) rounds, where \textOPT = \Theta(n/k+D) is the offline benchmark, and we show that there is no deterministic algorithm that admits an O(\textOPT)+o(E) guarantee. For the multiplicative distortion, we present several algorithms that induce a competitive ratio in \widetilde O(\min\ \sqrt\mu\log k, \mu\log\log k+\mu^1.5 ) , where the \widetilde O(\cdot) may hide logarithmic factors in \mu . [MA-9] Physical-Risk-Aware Decentralized Agents for Constrained Vehicle Communications

【速读】：该论文旨在解决去中心化车联网（V2X）资源控制中的核心挑战：在各车辆仅具备局部观测的情况下，如何实现全局网络可靠性与高效资源协同分配。其关键解决方案是提出一种面向物理风险的时空图注意力（PA-STGAT）框架，通过构建车辆局部交互图并保留短期时序上下文，使每辆车能够独立选择PC5链路资源与发射功率。训练阶段采用集中式策略优化，利用网络整体表现反馈来更新共享策略；通过发射端专属信用机制将可靠性失败追溯至具体车辆，并引入双价图项将网络违规转化为离散资源选择的协调信号，尤其优先保障短碰撞时间（time-to-collision）链接的通信质量。实验表明，该方法在原始策略可靠性上优于现有图神经网络基线，在过滤执行条件下可稳定满足五组随机种子下的通信需求，尽管存在效用、交付可靠性、资源占用与滤波修复之间的权衡。机制消融分析显示，若移除双价图协调机制，将导致资源选择崩溃。密度与移动性测试进一步揭示了该方法在实际约束满足能力上的运行边界。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17456
作者: Haoran Peng,William Weijia Zheng,Hang Liu,Ying-Jun Angela Zhang
机构: The Chinese University of Hong Kong (香港中文大学); University of Macau (澳门大学)
类目: ignal Processing (eess.SP); Multiagent Systems (cs.MA)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Decentralized V2X resource control is challenging because each vehicle has only local observations, while reliability depends on network-wide interactions. We develop a physical-risk-aware spatio-temporal graph attention (PA-STGAT) framework for agentic V2X control. Each vehicle perceives its local interaction graph, retains short-term temporal context, and independently selects its PC5 resource and power. Network outcomes are used during centralized training to refine the shared policy. Transmitter-specific credits map reliability failures to individual vehicles. A dual-priced graph term further converts network violations into coordination signals for discrete resource selection, with higher priority on short time-to-collision links. Experiments show higher raw-policy reliability than the tested graph-based baselines. Under filtered execution, PA-STGAT meets the nominal empirical communication requirements across all five seeds, with tradeoffs among utility, delivery reliability, resource occupancy, and filter-induced repair. Compared with GNN+MATD3, it requires fewer resource repairs but achieves lower utility and mean delivery reliability. Mechanism ablations show that removing dual-priced graph coordination causes resource-selection collapse. Density and mobility tests characterize the operating limits of empirical constraint satisfaction.

[NLP-0] ModHarness-RL: Recasting Harnesses as Learnable and Evolving Programs for Agent ic RL

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决生成式智能体在强化学习（Reinforcement Learning, RL）中依赖静态、人工设计的引导框架（harness）所导致的适应性不足问题。传统方法采用固定不变的手写框架，难以随模型训练过程及任务环境的变化动态调整，从而限制了智能体的表现。其解决方案的关键在于提出ModHarness-RL，将引导框架重构为可学习且可演化的程序形式：通过记录智能体交互过程为结构化状态（structured State），即一组带类型的块（typed blocks），并引入两个核心可学习组件——压缩程序（Compression program）用于将历史信息整合为模型上下文，以及引导程序（Steering program）在行为偏离时提供动态指导。在训练过程中，基于近期轨迹生成候选程序，经执行验证后回放至过往轨迹以评估效果，实现框架的持续演化。实验在CodeSearch、ALFWorld和BIRD SQL等任务上验证了该方法的有效性，结果显示，相较于固定框架基线，所提出的压缩与引导机制显著提升了模型在未见数据上的性能，表明面向生成式智能体的强化学习框架能够从任务数据中学习并伴随模型训练动态演化。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18533
作者: Zelin He,Siyu Zhu,Jacob Helwig,Yujian Liu,Li An,Rohit Jain,Shiyu Chang,Alborz Geramifard
机构: The Pennsylvania State University (宾夕法尼亚州立大学); LinkedIn (领英); University of California, Santa Barbara (加州大学圣塔芭芭拉分校)
类目: Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Machine Learning (cs.LG)
备注:

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Agentic reinforcement learning (RL) increasingly relies on a harness, the scaffold around the model that decides what it sees of its own interaction and what guidance it receives. Most existing approaches train under a hand-written harness that stays fixed throughout. However, an effective harness depends on the domain, the data and the model, and keeps changing as the model trains. We introduce ModHarness-RL, which recasts the harness as programs that are learned from data and evolve as the model trains with RL. ModHarness-RL records the interaction as structured State, a set of typed blocks, and two core components of the harness are programs over these blocks, a Compression program that assembles the accumulated history into the model’s context and a Steering program that supplies guidance when behavior deviates. During RL training, candidate programs are proposed from recent trajectories, verified by execution, and replayed over past trajectories, so the harness tracks how the model’s behavior shifts. We study CodeSearch in depth and further analyze ALFWorld and BIRD SQL, with models from 1.5B to 8B. The proposed Compression and Steering mechanisms improve held-out performance over the fixed-harness baseline in most settings. These results suggest that a harness for agentic RL can be learned from the task’s data and evolved with the model.

[NLP-1] WaferTetris: Efficient Freeform Mapping on Wafer-Scale Accelerators

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决晶圆级加速器（Wafer-Scale Accelerator, WSA）在大规模计算资源下应用映射效率低下的问题。由于其包含数十万处理单元（Processing Element, PE），传统模板化映射方法受限于固定的几何布局，导致计算与存储资源利用率不足。为突破这一瓶颈，本文提出WaferTetris系统，采用无约束的自由形式映射策略，通过类俄罗斯方块（Tetris-like）的灵活计算块打包机制，在不改变PE任务代码的前提下，显著提升处理单元利用率和可用内存容量。其关键技术在于构建基于递推关系的吞吐量模型以精确刻画分支-汇合依赖关系，指导空间并行性设计，并引入按颜色划分的门控架构模型，使现有FPGA布局布线算法可有效满足晶圆级芯片的布线约束。实验结果表明，在Cerebras WSE-3上对大语言模型（LLM）解码和科学型模板计算基准测试中，WaferTetris相较基于模板的自动映射基线最高实现4.42倍系统吞吐提升；相较于手动优化的WaferLLM，支持1.8倍更长上下文长度（80k vs 44k）的LLaMA3.1-8B模型，且在相同上下文长度下保持相当或更高的每用户吞吐量（最高达1.10倍）。

Abstract:Wafer-scale accelerators offer massive compute capacity through hundreds of thousands of processing elements (PEs) interconnected across a single wafer, but realizing this potential requires efficient application mapping. Their scale makes programming these systems and mapping applications onto them challenging. Existing approaches rely on template-based mappings to maintain productivity, but these templates restrict the mapping design space. In particular, they impose fixed geometric layouts over the PE grid, often leaving compute and memory resources underutilized. In this work, we present WaferTetris, a system that removes these geometric restrictions through freeform mapping. Its Tetris-like strategy flexibly packs computation across the wafer, improving PE utilization and usable memory capacity while preserving PE-task code. We develop a recurrence-based throughput model that captures branch-and-join dependencies to guide spatial parallelism, and an architecture model with per-color gates that enable existing FPGA place-and-route algorithms to enforce WSE routing constraints. We evaluate WaferTetris on Cerebras WSE-3 using LLM decoding and scientific stencil benchmarks. For LLM decoding, it achieves up to 4.42\times the system throughput of a template-based automatic mapping baseline on the same fabric size. Compared with the manually optimized WaferLLM, WaferTetris supports 1.8\times the context length for LLaMA3.1-8B (80k versus 44k), while delivering equal or higher per-user throughput at matched context lengths, up to 1.10\times . [NLP-2] Can Mechanistic Interpretability Help Explain LLM Agent Failures? A Case Study on Web Element Hallucination

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决生成式人工智能（Generative AI）在解释大语言模型（Large Language Models, LLMs）驱动的智能体（LLM-based agents）决策过程中的局限性问题，特别是针对智能体在实际应用中出现的“幻觉行为”——即在网页操作中生成不存在的动作。其核心挑战在于现有机制可解释性（Mechanistic Interpretability, MI）方法难以有效揭示此类失败的根本原因。论文的关键解决方案是通过一系列机制分析，发现对最后一个提示词（prompt token）进行线性探测可实现对幻觉行为的早期预测（AUC=0.80），并利用探测方向进行干预可纠正85%的幻觉行为，但会误伤7%的有效动作。然而，该方法仅提供部分解释：探测方向对提示格式高度敏感，微小的表示变化即导致其控制效果显著下降；同时，相关计算路径分散于多个组件与位置，且依赖信息随样本动态变化，使得电路识别困难。这些发现挑战了传统MI方法在电路发现与特征定位中的核心假设，提示未来需发展更鲁棒、适应性强的可解释性框架以应对复杂智能体系统中的失效模式。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18514
作者: Mohamed Aghzal,Mor Geva,Gregory J. Stein,Ziyu Yao
机构: George Mason University(乔治梅森大学); Tel Aviv University(特拉维夫大学)
类目: Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL)
备注:

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Mechanistic Interpretability (MI) has made significant progress in explaining the internal computations of Large Language Models (LLMs); however, these advances have yet to demonstrate comparable success in explaining the decision-making processes of LLM-based agents. This paper examines the challenges MI tools face when applied to realistic agentic failures. We focus on one such failure: web agents hallucinating actions that are not available on a website. We apply a series of mechanistic analyzes to Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct as it interacts with live websites. We find that a linear probe trained on the last prompt token can predict hallucinations before generation begins (0.80 AUC) and steering along a probe direction corrects 85% of hallucinations, but disrupts 7% of valid actions. However, these results only provide a partial explanation of why the agent hallucinates. The probe direction is sensitive to prompt formatting, losing much of its steering effect after a minor change to how actions are represented. Additionally, circuit identification can be difficult as the computation is distributed across multiple components and positions, and the information the agent relies on varies across examples. We discuss how these observations challenge the assumptions underlying standard MI methods for circuit discovery and feature identification, and outline future directions for making these methods better suited to explain failures in LLM-based agents.

[NLP-3] Agent ic induction of common data elements and information extraction from sarcoma case report abstracts

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决临床数据可比性中通用数据元素（Common Data Elements, CDEs）定义依赖人工、效率低且难以规模化的问题。其核心解决方案在于利用大语言模型代理（large language model agents）从软组织肉瘤病例报告摘要中自动推导CDE模式，并实现大规模应用。关键创新点在于通过代理生成大量候选CDE（72,909个），经多轮智能整合后提炼出1,570个候选，再经文献证据支持筛选保留53个高频出现的CDE；随后在全语料库中进行证据关联抽取，整体处理成功率达79.2%，且在100份抽样摘要中与人工标注结果的精确匹配度高达94.9%（95%置信区间：93.0%-96.4%）。尽管在解剖学和时间相关字段仍存在误差，但结果表明，基于代理辅助的初始模式生成结合专家校验，可有效支撑大规模、标准化的临床数据提取流程。

Abstract:Common data elements (CDEs) support clinical data comparability but are usually defined manually. We investigated whether large language model agents could derive a CDE schema from sarcoma case report abstracts and apply it at scale. An agent proposed 72,909 candidate CDEs from 24,588 PubMed abstracts; agentic consolidation yielded 1,570 CDEs. We retained 53 supported by at least 50 abstracts and attempted evidence-linked extraction across the corpus. Processing succeeded for all retained CDEs in 79.2% of abstracts, including responses indicating non-mention. Two human reviewers approved 83.0% of retained CDEs for relevance, 67.9% for clarity, and 56.6% for correctness. Errors concentrated in anatomical and temporal fields. These findings support agent-assisted schema drafting followed by expert curation before large-scale extraction.

[NLP-4] Interactive Program Induction with Language Model Priors

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决程序归纳（Program Induction）中的根本性歧义问题：从有限的输入-输出样例中恢复出目标程序时，理论上存在无限多个与已知样例一致但对未见输入产生不同输出的程序。为克服这一挑战，论文从贝叶斯视角出发，将程序归纳建模为在观测到额外输入-输出样例后，使后验概率集中在真实目标程序 $ f^\star $ 上的过程。其核心解决方案是提出一种交互式程序归纳框架，其中学习者通过主动选择信息量最大的查询输入，向可信源（如用户）请求输出反馈，并基于此逐步缩小程序空间。关键创新在于设计了一种贪心查询选择算法，在假设先验 $ p^0 $ 仅支持唯一最优程序类的前提下，可在 $ \mathcal{O}(\frac{1}{\gamma} \log \frac{1}{p^0(f^\star)}) $ 次查询内收敛至目标程序（至近似等价）。该方法采用语言模型作为先验，并在类似 PAC（Probably Approximately Correct）的程序等价性松弛框架下，利用重要性采样估计查询选择目标。在 CodeARC 基准测试中，该算法相较仅依赖语言模型的代理，准确率提升最高达 22 个百分点，且最多减少 10.5 个输入-输出样例的观测需求。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18485
作者: Robin Shing Moon Chan,Ryan Cotterell
机构: ETH Zürich(苏黎世联邦理工学院)
类目: Computation and Language (cs.CL); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Machine Learning (cs.LG)
备注: Preprint. 28 pages, 5 figures, 9 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Program induction refers to the process in which a learner aims to recover a program from a few given input-output examples. This paradigm broadens programming access by letting users demonstrate the rule-based behavior they want to apply to unseen data, instead of having to implement it themselves. However, recovering a program from finite input-output examples is fundamentally ambiguous, as a finite set of input-output examples is theoretically consistent with infinitely many programs whose output diverges on unseen inputs. We view this problem from a Bayesian perspective, where, given a prior p^0 over programs, identifying the target program means concentrating posterior probability mass on it by observing additional input-output examples. In the interactive program induction setting, the learner chooses maximally informative inputs and queries an oracle, such as the user, for their outputs. We derive a greedy query-selection algorithm and show that it terminates after \mathcalO(\frac1\gamma\log 1/p^0(f^\star)) informative oracle queries, and returns the target program f^\star up to approximate equivalence when no other class outweighs it under the prior. The algorithm uses a language model as the prior under a PAC-style relaxation of program equivalence, where the query-selection objective is estimated using importance sampling. On the CodeARC benchmark, the algorithm improves accuracy over the LM only agent by up to 22 percentage points while observing up to 10.5 fewer input-output examples.

[NLP-5] Do Verifier Judgments Survive Abstraction? Measuring Verification Transport for LLM Agent Evaluation NEURIPS2026

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决大语言模型（LLM）作为验证器（verifier）在评估智能体（agent）执行过程时，其判断可靠性在不同执行可观测性抽象层级下是否保持稳定的问题。随着智能体系统在实际部署中常通过工具组合、任务委托边界或历史信息压缩等方式对执行轨迹进行抽象，验证器所见的执行证据也随之改变，但现有评估方法通常仅基于单一表示形式，忽视了这种抽象带来的可观测性变化对验证结果的影响。为此，论文提出“验证传输”（Verification Transport）这一可靠性测试框架，通过构建同一冻结执行的原生视图、抽象视图与还原视图，在保持任务、轨迹、结果、真实标签、评估标准及验证器不变的前提下，系统性地检验验证判断在抽象边界下的稳定性。实验覆盖四个基准数据集、七种验证器模型、四种验证协议及三类抽象边界，发现高达约31%的验证判断在宏观抽象和委托场景中出现“传输失败”（transport failure），且不同验证协议表现出差异化的失效模式：直接验证更易产生错误结论，而基于证据和反思型协议则倾向于在可观测性受限时选择弃权。研究进一步表明，较低的传输失败率并不等同于更高的可靠性。通过恢复执行溯源信息，可挽回85.5%的传输失败案例，但当溯源信息丢失或不完整时，恢复效果显著下降。这揭示了仅依赖原生视图准确率无法全面刻画验证器在跨抽象边界场景下的可靠性，强调了执行溯源在保障验证一致性中的关键作用。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18471
作者: Disha Sheshanarayana,Sai Linisha Reddy Goli
机构: Manipal University Jaipur(曼达普大学贾伊普尔分校)
类目: Computation and Language (cs.CL); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)
备注: Accepted at NeurIPS 2026 Workshop on Who Verifies the Agents? Toward Reliable Agent Development

点击查看摘要

Abstract:LLM-based verifiers are increasingly used to evaluate agent executions, but their reliability is typically measured under a single representation of the trajectory. In deployed agent systems, the same execution may be exposed through composed tools, delegation boundaries, or compacted histories, changing what execution evidence is directly observable to the verifier. It therefore remains unclear whether correct verifier judgments persist across such abstraction boundaries. We introduce Verification Transport, a reliability test that measures judgment stability under abstraction-induced changes in execution observability. We construct matched native, abstracted, and restored views of the same frozen executions while holding the task, realized trajectory, outcome, ground truth, evaluation criterion, and verifier fixed. Across four agent-evaluation benchmarks, seven verifier backbones, four verification protocols, and three abstraction boundaries, we find substantial transport failure, reaching about 31% under macro abstraction and delegation. Verification protocols exhibit distinct failure modes: direct verification more often produces incorrect verdicts, whereas evidence-grounded and deliberative protocols tend to abstain under limited observability. This suggests that lower transport failure rates do not necessarily imply greater reliability. Restoring the corresponding execution provenance recovers 85.5% of transport failures, while recovery is lower when provenance is lossy or unavailable. These results show that native-view accuracy alone is insufficient to characterize verifier reliability when execution evidence crosses abstraction boundaries.

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决生成式模型在捕捉文学阅读过程中人类情感动态演变（affective trajectory）时，其预测与读者情感响应之间对齐（alignment）的解释性问题。现有研究常将这种对齐归因于叙事位置、情感词汇或文本特异性读者反应机制，但缺乏区分这些因素的能力。为此，作者提出LitArc-Bench基准，通过39段中文文学节选文本，按七个时间点划分评估节点，并基于81名成年参与者在两个班级中完成的430份问卷（共9,020次VAD评分），系统比较三种“未来不可知”模型运行方式：基于位置先验、词法情感特征以及人类间参照。研究发现，仅依赖位置信息即可达到唤醒度（arousal）的相关系数r = .470，表明原始对齐程度不足以揭示深层机制。然而，在所有六种情感维度转换（V/A transition）中，相对于联合位置-词典控制组，其优势均显著且置信区间高于零。交叉拟合模型在全部九个模型维度单元中，均能显著优于其余38个不匹配文本（p < .0001），而文本无关的负向控制组则表现随机（p = 1）。当人类情感变化偏离位置趋势至少0.5分时，模型仍可恢复69–76%的效价方向与53–68%的唤醒及支配方向。在跨被试-文本重采样下，所有九个身份区间仍显著高于零，联合控制的优势在效价和方向性上稳健，但在唤醒方面较弱。尽管模型在形态一致性（shape agreement）上表现良好，但仍存在量级偏差（scale bias）。综上，LitArc-Bench揭示了文学读者反应中可被模型捕获的成分，强调需将形态、校准与人类变异性的评估进行分离，以超越浅层解释。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18412
作者: Mingming Su,Yushan Wei,Sen Song,Xinche Zhang

Abstract:When model predictions track an unfolding human affect trajectory, alignment may reflect narrative position, affective words, or text-specific reader-response dynamics. We introduce LitArc-Bench to separate these explanations: 39 Chinese-language literary excerpts segmented into seven checkpoints, with 430 questionnaires from 81 adults in two classroom groups (9,020 VAD ratings). We compare three future-blind model runs with leave-one-text-out position priors, lexical affect, and human-human references. Position alone reaches r = .470 for arousal, making raw alignment insufficient. Yet all six V/A transition advantages over a joint position-lexicon control have text-bootstrap intervals above zero. Cross-fitted models match the correct human text better than 38 mismatched texts in all nine model-dimension cells (p .0001); a text-invariant negative control returns chance (p = 1). On human transitions at least 0.5 points against the position trend, models recover 69-76% of valence directions and 53-68% for arousal and dominance. Under crossed participant-text resampling, all nine identity intervals remain above zero; joint-control advantages are robust for valence and directional but less certain for arousal. High shape agreement still coexists with scale bias. LitArc-Bench identifies which components of literary reader response are recoverable beyond shallow explanations and why shape, calibration, and human variability require separate evaluation.

Abstract:We introduce Gamer, a generalist agent framework built for long-horizon tasks in complex interactive environments. Its end-to-end architecture combines perception, memory management, and multi-step reasoning. A shared training and deployment pipeline supports interaction with both conventional 2D graphical interfaces and complex 3D simulated worlds. We first continue pre-training on a dataset constructed from tens of thousands of hours of device-level keyboard and mouse interaction. These trajectories span simulated games and embodied environments, allowing the model to learn environmental dynamics across both physical and virtual domains. Sparse-thinking instruction tuning then substantially reduces redundant computation while preserving the reasoning needed for complex decisions. These results suggest that even repetitive keyboard and mouse trajectories can support substantial gains from scaling data and learning algorithms. This approach offers a path toward generalist agents that can interact across digital and physical worlds.

[NLP-9] f-Divergence Self-Play for Tabular Anomaly Detection via Large Language Models ICML2026

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决表格数据中异常检测（anomaly detection）的挑战，尤其针对异构特征类型（混合数值型、类别型和文本型属性）导致的正常模式表征学习困难问题。现有基于大语言模型（Large Language Models, LLMs）的方法虽将表格行序列化为文本序列进行处理，但依赖于一次性监督微调，难以充分捕捉正常数据的分布特性。本文提出DiSPaT——一种自对弈微调（self-play fine-tuning）框架，其核心在于通过最小化f-散度（f-divergence）的理论基础，构建一个紧致的近似目标，以缩小真实正常数据与模型生成样本之间的分布差距。DiSPaT采用交替优化机制：在每轮迭代中，当前策略模型生成合成样本作为伪异常，由判别器（critic discriminator）学习区分这些样本与真实正常样本；判别器提供的反馈信号驱动策略模型更新，逐步使模型生成的数据分布逼近真实正常数据分布。实验结果表明，DiSPaT在多个基准测试中显著优于现有的基于LLM的方法、深度学习模型及经典无监督检测器，在表格异常检测任务中展现出更强的性能与鲁棒性。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18370
作者: Hoang Tran Vuong,Linh Ngo Van,Dang Nguyen,Thin Nguyen,Phuoc Nguyen,Mehrtash Harandi,Trung Le
机构: Hanoi University of Science and Technology (河内科技大学); Deakin University (迪肯大学); Monash University (蒙纳士大学)
类目: Machine Learning (cs.LG); Computation and Language (cs.CL)
备注: Accepted at ICML 2026. 28 pages, 7 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Anomaly detection in tabular data poses significant challenges due to heterogeneous feature types-mixing numerical, categorical, and textual attributes, which complicate learning meaningful representations of normality. Recent work has applied large language models (LLMs) to this problem by serializing table rows as text sequences, yet these approaches rely on one-shot supervised fine-tuning that offers limited signal to tighten the model’s description of normality. We propose DiSPaT, a self-play fine-tuning framework that strengthens the model’s understanding of normal data. Building on the theoretical foundation of f-divergence minimization, we derive a tight approximation connecting our training objective to reducing the distributional gap between real normal data and model-generated samples. DiSPaT operates through an alternating optimization: at each iteration, the current policy generates synthetic samples that serve as pseudo-anomalies, while a critic discriminator learns to distinguish these from real normal samples; this signal drives policy updates that progressively align the model distribution with the true normal-data distribution. Extensive experiments on diverse benchmarks demonstrate that DiSPaT consistently outperforms prior LLM-based methods, deep learning approaches, and classical unsupervised detectors for tabular anomaly detection.

Abstract:The release of GPT-6 Astra marks the emergence of a new paradigm in robotics: agentic robotics. Within weeks, frontier models with agentic harnesses matched or exceeded task-specific policies on many embodied benchmarks. Instead of training a dedicated policy for each family of tasks, an agentic robotic system applies a foundation model to open-world embodied tasks. It operates in a closed loop: observing the environment, interpreting user intent, reasoning about the task, invoking skills and tools, verifying outcomes, and adapting its strategy. Despite these advances, the community has yet to converge on a shared definition, a reliable evaluation framework, or a research roadmap. We argue that Embodied System 2, realized by a foundation model together with an embodied harness, forms the decision-making core of agentic robotics. Its central challenge is to translate general-purpose reasoning into effective and verifiable actions under physical constraints such as limited reaction time, mechanical limits, unpredictable environment changes, and irreversible consequences. Motivated by this perspective, we propose an evaluation framework that measures both model capabilities and closed-loop system performance. Furthermore, we identify the principles that carry over from digital to physical agents and the challenges that must be addressed to achieve reliable physical autonomy. On top of the Embodied System 2 and the evaluation framework, we envision a research roadmap toward sustained autonomy: the ability to perform work safely and reliably with progressively broader responsibility, from executing individual primitives to fulfilling standing roles.

[NLP-11] Learning Word-Level Explanations of Text Reuse in Latin Literature with Little Annotation

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决古典文献学中文本重用（text reuse）分析的深层问题，即在识别出文本间的互文关系后，进一步揭示后世作者如何具体重构原始文本。其核心挑战在于对重用片段进行逐词层面的操作标注：将重用句中的每个词与源文本中的对应词对齐，并标记其转换操作类型（如直接复制、词形变化或替换）。此类标注成本高昂，现有拉丁文学文本重用语料库中仅有极少数具备词级标注。为此，论文提出一种高效解决方案：利用已知操作对拉丁文生成合成训练样本以初步训练模型，再通过主动学习（active learning）策略选择最具信息量的样本交由人工标注。实验表明，在多种模型架构中，联合执行词对齐与操作标注的指针网络（pointer network）表现最优。结果显示，仅需200个基于不确定性的样本标注，即可使模型性能达到使用全部1,490个参考文献标注数据训练模型的水平。研究同时开源代码、所有参考文献的词级预测结果及交互式演示系统。

Abstract:Detecting an intertextual reference is only the first step; philologists also need to know how a later author reworked the source. We explain a reuse pair word by word: each word of the reusing passage is first aligned to its source word, then labeled with the operation that transformed it, such as a copy, a change of inflection, or a substitution. Such word-level operations are expensive to annotate, and only a few corpora annotated at this level exist for text reuse in Latin literature. We describe a procedure that needs only a limited number of annotations: synthetic pairs, built by applying known operations to Latin text, train a first model, and active learning chooses the pairs an annotator corrects. Among several architectures, a model that links and labels together performs best: a pointer network that, for every reuse word, chooses a source word and names the operation in the same step. We show that with active learning, 200 pairs chosen by uncertainty are enough for the model to match one trained on every annotated pair of the 1,490 references of the Loci Similes benchmark. We release our code, word-level predictions for all references, and an interactive demo.

[NLP-12] When evaluation becomes intervention in clinical AI

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决在临床人工智能（Clinical AI）系统中，由生成式AI（Generative AI）作为评判者（AI judge）对其他系统输出进行评分时所引发的潜在偏差问题。当团队利用评判者的评估结果来选择提示词（prompt）或训练模型时，评判者实际上已成为干预过程的一部分，其自身存在的评估盲点可能导致错误被系统性地偏好和放大。解决方案的关键在于明确评判报告必须包含的内容，并要求所有基于评判者驱动的改动均需通过独立的临床测试进行验证，以确保干预措施的有效性与安全性，避免引入不可察觉的偏差。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18297
作者: Anthony Abou Mrad,Aviona Conti,Samuel Schmidgall,Jayanth Pratap,Ruhika Iyer,Joseph Kim,King Shi,Yann Sakref,Luis M. Ahumada,Lisa Knowlton,Andrew M. Cameron,Khyzer Aziz,Jeffrey K. Jopling

类目: Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Computers and Society (cs.CY)
备注: 12 pages, 3 figures, 1 box. Submitted to npj Digital Medicine

点击查看摘要

Abstract:AI judges, models that grade another system’s output against a rubric, extend clinical review to the scale at which clinical AI writes. When teams use judge assessments to select a prompt or train a model, the judge becomes part of the intervention. Selection can then favor errors the judge overlooks. We describe what a judge’s report must contain and the independent clinical test each judge-driven change needs.

[NLP-13] GT-FENG: General Theory of Factual Evaluation and Normative Grounding in Language Models *Author(s)

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决语言模型在法律、政策及其他规则驱动领域中答案正确性评估的难题，即答案的正确性高度依赖于具体语境（如司法管辖区、时间点或法律定义），而现有评估方法难以准确区分因上下文差异导致的正确性变化与模型本身产生的错误。其核心解决方案是提出首个统一框架——通用事实评估与规范依据理论（GT-FENG），该框架通过将模型输出与适用规则进行比对，识别出与错误答案匹配的已记录规则，并揭示当多个规则产生相同结果时，仅凭答案无法判断模型所依据的具体规则。实验表明，在医疗补助收入限额的问答任务中，引用目标州自身记录可使仅与其他州数值匹配的错误答案减少68%-78%，且超过一半的稳定错误答案可被两个或更多已记录来源支持。这一方法为规则驱动领域中语言模型的评估提供了可解释、可验证的基础，并推动了未来基准测试与评估工具包的发展。研究团队已将GT-FENG开源为Python工具包（gtfeng）。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18279
作者: Jiayu Feng
机构: Harvard T.H. Chan School of Public Health (哈佛大学陈曾熙公共卫生学院)
类目: Computation and Language (cs.CL)
备注: 7 pages, 4 tables. Code: this https URL

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Whether a language model’s answer is correct often depends on context: the same value can be right under one jurisdiction, date, or legal definition and wrong under another. We introduce GT-FENG (General Theory of Factual Evaluation and Normative Grounding), the first general framework to unify contextual/temporal correctness and rule-based error attribution across law, policy, and other rule-governed domains. GT-FENG checks answers against the applicable rules and identifies documented rules that match incorrect answers. It shows that when multiple rules give the same value, the answer alone cannot tell which rule the model used. In Medicaid income-limit answers from two models, documenting the asked state’s own records reduces the number of wrong answers that match only other states’ values by 68-78%, and more than half of stable wrong answers then match two or more documented sources. GT-FENG provides a foundation for evaluating language models in rule-governed domains and for future benchmarks and evaluation toolkits. We release GT-FENG as an open-source Python package, gtfeng.

[NLP-14] MamaReason : Unfinished Reasoning and Answer Recovery in Clinical Language Models

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决生成式 AI 在临床推理任务中存在“重复诊断”（recurrent diagnosis）的问题，即模型在未完成推理过程时便提前输出已陈述的诊断结论，导致推理链（reasoning trace）的有效性被削弱。其解决方案的关键在于引入一种显式承诺提取器（explicit-commitment extractor），通过识别推理过程中首次出现的诊断承诺（diagnosis commitment），并基于诊断字符串进行匹配分析，从而将诊断内容、推理完成度与配对诊断正确性三者解耦。研究发现，在强化学习（RL）和监督微调（SFT）的失败首轮推理中，75%以上的错误案例均存在可提取的诊断承诺，且其后续续写内容与承诺高度一致。进一步通过离线二次承诺触发机制，量化了早期读出（early readout）与配对准确率之间的权衡关系，揭示出不同读出策略对诊断正确性的独立影响。这一分解框架使临床语言模型评估从单一的“最终答案正确性”转向多维度的可解释性分析，凸显了同一推理轨迹在多重读出视角下的差异化表现，为评估生成式 AI 在复杂临床决策中的可靠性提供了新范式。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18264
作者: Thanni Adewuyi,Adepoju Jeremiah Moyondafoluwa,Dorcas Amapu,Dieko Whenu,Sylvia Essessien,Sandra Oko-Oba,Pelumi Onafadeji,Lily Aimas-Love,Samuel Omolaiye,Abiodun Adereni
机构: HelpMum Africa
类目: Computation and Language (cs.CL)
备注:

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Answer continuation often returns a diagnosis already stated in unfinished clinical reasoning. We study this behavior in MamaReason, adapted from dense Gemma 4 31B, on MamaBench’s 217 paired maternal and child-health presentations. An explicit-commitment extractor identifies a diagnosis in 75 of 94 failed RL first passes and 75 of 93 failed SFT first passes. The continuation matches that diagnosis in 67/75 RL cases and 58/75 SFT cases, using diagnosis strings alone. Reading these commitments without additional generation recovers 30 of the 40 RL pairs restored by continuation under the archived correctness labels. Recurrence controls distinguish routine mentions from repeated answer selection. An offline second-commitment trigger measures the tradeoff between earlier readout and paired accuracy. The resulting decomposition separates diagnostic content already present in a trace, completion of an accepted answer, and paired diagnostic correctness. For clinical language-model evaluation, these are distinct outcomes that become visible when the same reasoning trace is examined through multiple readouts.

Abstract:The automated transformation of unstructured natural language process descriptions into Business Process Model and Notation (BPMN) diagrams remains a challenging task, due to linguistic ambiguity and the difficulty to detect non-adjacent control-flow relationships. [NLP-17] Folding Language Models into Themselves

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决知识蒸馏（Knowledge Distillation）中学生模型（student）通常需从零开始大规模训练的问题，尽管教师模型（teacher）的权重已包含大部分可迁移知识。其核心挑战在于如何高效利用教师模型的参数，避免冗余训练并保持多维度能力的全面保留。解决方案的关键在于提出一种名为Concertina的新架构：将教师模型中每N个相邻层折叠为一个共享块（shared block），通过循环执行该块实现层数压缩；每个循环步引入基于原层初始化的低秩修正（low-rank correction），并结合逐标记门控机制（per-token gate）动态融合这些修正，使每个输入标记能够从单一共享块中重构原始多层行为。此外，设计了一种平衡通用、数学、学术及特定选择题数据的蒸馏数据配方，以协同恢复教师模型在多种任务上的综合性能。实验表明，在Qwen3-4B教师模型上，仅用220亿个令牌训练的22.7亿参数Concertina模型，在常识与数学推理任务上保留约90%性能，平均在八个基准测试中达到84%，显著优于同等规模的密集型与层共享基线模型，甚至超越训练数据量超千倍的Qwen3-1.7B模型。结果表明，通过将教师模型自身结构折叠并配合优化数据策略，可无需大规模重训练即恢复其绝大部分能力，为轻量化模型构建提供了高效新范式。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18113
作者: Ziyang Zhang,Yubin Jing,Yuyao Li,Haofan Wang,Yichen Gong
机构: 未知
类目: Computation and Language (cs.CL)
备注: 14 pages, 4 figures, 9 tables. Code: this https URL

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Knowledge distillation usually trains a compact student from scratch, which needs a large amount of data even though the teacher’s weights already encode most of what the student has to learn. In this work, we explore building the student directly from the teacher’s weights. We propose Concertina, which folds every group of N adjacent teacher layers into one shared block that is executed N times. Each loop step carries a low-rank correction initialized from the layer it replaces, and a per-token gate mixes these corrections, to let every token recover the behavior of the original layers from a single shared block. We further design a distillation data recipe that balances general, mathematical, academic and targeted multiple-choice data, to recover all capabilities of the teacher together rather than one at the expense of another. On Qwen3-4B, a 2.27B Concertina model distilled on 22B tokens retains about 90% of the teacher on commonsense and mathematical reasoning and 84% on average across eight benchmarks. It outperforms same-size dense and layer-sharing baselines, as well as Qwen3-1.7B, which is trained on over a thousand times more data. These results suggest that a compact model need not be retrained at scale, since folding the teacher into itself and distilling it on the right data recovers most of its capabilities. Code is available at this https URL.

[NLP-18] Contagious Fingerprints: When LLM s Inherit Another Models Identity

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决大语言模型（Large Language Model, LLM）文本指纹在实际应用中被“传染”导致的可检测性失效问题。传统方法假设模型指纹是固定的，但研究发现，当一个模型接续由另一模型发起的对话时，会继承前者的指纹特征，从而使得基于指纹的模型识别技术失效。这一现象严重威胁了API代理服务的审计机制——攻击者可通过将初始对话交由高成本模型处理、后续阶段切换至低成本模型的方式实现隐蔽替换，而现有分类器对这种替换的检测率显著下降（如DeepSeek替换Claude Sonnet时从0.97降至0.72）。研究进一步揭示，指纹传染源于模型对历史对话风格的模仿，且历史依赖性使模型输出偏离自身原始指纹，导致基于无历史参考的检测方法（如标准MMD测试）出现误判或漏检。为此，作者提出\textscLineup方案，其核心创新在于采用与待测回复相同历史上下文的、来自声称模型的多个参考样本进行对比，并通过计算待测回复在参考集中的相对排名来判断其真实性。该方法通过交换性（exchangeability）控制假阳性率，无需人工设定阈值或依赖对数概率，实验证明其在两个封闭模型上的假阳性率为0.056（预注册边界为0.10），并在六组替换场景中于四轮审计后实现了0.81的检测率，显著提升了对抗指纹传染和模型伪装的鲁棒性。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18104
作者: Zailong Tian
机构: Nanyang Technological University (南洋理工大学)
类目: Computation and Language (cs.CL)
备注:

点击查看摘要

Abstract:The fingerprint by which a classifier recognizes the LLM behind a text is usually treated as a fixed property of the model. We show that it is contagious: a model that continues a conversation begun by another model inherits that model’s fingerprint. This undermines audits of API relays, which can serve the first turns of a conversation with the model a customer pays for and silently hand the rest to a cheaper one. After such a handoff, detection of the substitute by a text classifier drops from 0.97 to 0.72 (DeepSeek replacing Claude Sonnet) and from 0.87 to 0.54 for a same-vendor downgrade (Haiku), and retraining on handoff data repairs only substitutes seen in training. Preregistered experiments trace the contagion to imitation of the history’s style. History also moves a model away from its own fingerprint: active probes are not camouflaged, but when calibrated on fresh conversations they flag \emphhonest models on long contexts at rates up to 1.00, and a standard MMD equality test rejects all nine models we test. Both failures come from comparing a history-dependent reply with a history-free reference. We propose \textscLineup, which samples references from the claimed model \emphon the same history and tests the audited reply by its rank among them. \textscLineup controls false positives by exchangeability and needs no fitted threshold or log-probabilities. With contemporaneous references on two closed models, its false-positive rate is 0.056 (preregistered bound 0.10), and it detects 0.81 of substitute sessions across six pairs after four audited turns.

[NLP-19] VEPO: Mitigating Multilingual Hallucinations in LVLMs via Visual Evidence-guided Preference Optimization

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决大视觉语言模型（Large Vision-Language Models, LVLMs）在低资源语言中普遍存在且严重的对象幻觉（object hallucination）问题，即模型生成在语言上流畅但缺乏视觉依据的错误回答。这一现象在低资源语言中尤为突出，因其跨模态对齐能力较弱，导致模型过度依赖语言先验而非图像证据。现有解决方案或需昂贵的多语言标注数据、外部翻译/奖励模型，或在推理阶段干预，可能扭曲输出语言的自然性。本文提出一种训练阶段的方法——视觉证据引导的偏好优化（Visual Evidence-guided Preference Optimization, VEPO），通过将英语中的可靠视觉定位能力迁移至其他语言，无需依赖外部模型。VEPO首先利用英语查询通过中间层跨模态注意力机制定位关键视觉证据，进而构建偏好样本对：两者具有相同正确答案，但一个使用完整图像，另一个仅保留关键区域（其余区域被掩码）。基于这些样本进行直接偏好优化（Direct Preference Optimization），促使低资源语言输入更倾向于选择由完整视觉证据支持的答案。在包含中文、法语和印地语的多语言POPE与MME基准测试中，实验结果表明VEPO能持续降低幻觉率并提升感知性能，尤其在最低资源语言上表现最佳。分析进一步证实，VEPO增强了问题相关的注意力聚焦，且基于视觉证据的掩码策略优于随机掩码。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18096
作者: Chengpeng Fu,Xiaocheng Feng,Wenshuai Huo,Yichong Huang,Baohang Li,Duyu Tang,Ting Liu
机构: 未知
类目: Computation and Language (cs.CL)
备注:

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Large vision-language models (LVLMs) achieve strong multimodal performance, yet they remain prone to object hallucination—generating answers that are linguistically fluent but visually ungrounded. This issue is especially severe in low-resource languages, where weaker cross-modal alignment causes models to rely more on language priors than on image evidence. Existing remedies either require costly multilingual annotations or external translation/reward models, or intervene at inference time in ways that can distort the output language. We propose VEPO(Visual Evidence-guided Preference Optimization), a training-time method that transfers reliable visual grounding from English to other languages without external models. VEPO first localizes key visual evidence using English queries via mid-layer cross-modal attention, then constructs preference pairs that share the same correct answer but differ in evidence sufficiency (full image vs.\ key-region masked image). Direct preference optimization on these pairs encourages low-resource inputs to prefer answers supported by complete visual evidence. Experiments on multilingual POPE and MME with Chinese, French, and Hindi show consistent hallucination reduction and perception gains, with the largest improvements on the lowest-resource language. Analyses further confirm that VEPO sharpens question-relevant attention and that evidence-guided masking outperforms random masking.

[NLP-20] Deep Latents Long Memory: Latent persistence explains temporal correlations in transformer activations

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决大语言模型（Large Language Models, LLMs）中隐藏层表征抽象性缺乏可量化评估标准的问题。由于当前尚无操作性定义来衡量“抽象性”，导致难以实证检验深层表征是否随网络深度提升而实现更高层次的抽象。为此，作者提出一种基于序列相关性的新视角：生成过程的结构会反映在数据序列的统计相关性中。研究定义了每一层的自相关函数（autocorrelation function），即通过计算同一层在相隔特定滞后（lag）的两个上下文位置激活向量间的平均点积来衡量。结果显示，在合成数据与真实文本数据中，随着网络深度增加，自相关函数的衰减变慢，呈现出更长的尾部特征。这种尾部增长源于对高层级潜变量（latent variables）的编码，这些潜变量控制着输入中较长的上下文跨度。在具有可控层级潜结构的合成数据中，潜变量的持久性（persistence）能够准确预测自相关的定性模式，并解释绝大多数观测到的度量结果。在真实文本中，研究采用稀疏自编码器（sparse-autoencoder）提取的特征作为潜变量的代理，发现其持久性与自相关幅度在不同数据集和模型间高度相关；且潜变量的持久时间随深度递增，表明深层特征能维持更长的上下文跨度。进一步的消融实验验证了这一结论：移除具有持久性的稀疏自编码器特征后，自相关显著下降。因此，该研究的关键突破在于：以潜变量的持久性为中介，建立了一种可量化、可验证的抽象性指标，从而揭示了深层表征随深度增强抽象能力的核心机制。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18089
作者: Francesco Cagnetta,Santiago Acevedo
机构: Simplex; SISSA(国际高等研究院)
类目: Machine Learning (cs.LG); Computation and Language (cs.CL)
备注:

点击查看摘要

Abstract:The hidden layers of Large Language Models (LLMs) are believed to encode representations of the data whose abstraction increases with depth. However, in the absence of an operational notion of abstraction, this statement is hard to test quantitatively. Here, we propose an alternative view, building on the principle that the correlations of a sequence reflect the structure of the process that generated it. We define the autocorrelation function of a layer as the average dot product between its activations at two context positions separated by a lag. Latent variables encoded persistently along the context create slowly decaying tails in the autocorrelation, and we find these tails to grow across layers in both synthetic and natural data. In synthetic data with controlled hierarchical latent structure, this growth stems from the encoding of abstract latent variables, which govern long stretches of the input. Indeed, the persistence of latent codes predicts the qualitative structure of autocorrelations and accounts for most of the empirical measures. In real text, we take sparse-autoencoder features as proxies for latent variables, and find that their persistence correlates strongly with the magnitude of autocorrelations across datasets and LLMs. In particular, the persistence time increases with depth, as do autocorrelations, implying that deeper latent features persist for longer context spans. We complement this analysis by explicitly ablating the persistent sparse-autoencoder features and observing a consequent decrease in the autocorrelations.

[NLP-21] Agent -as-a-Judge

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决当前大语言模型（LLM）评估体系中“被动评判者”（passive judge）因依赖静态输入信息而无法获取缺失证据的问题，尤其在评估智能体轨迹、可执行代码或领域特定报告时，关键环境状态或争议性事实可能未被记录，导致判断不可靠。其解决方案的关键在于提出“智能体即评判者”（Agent-as-a-Judge）框架，通过引入主动式评估机制，使评判者能够动态构建并控制验证过程，主动检索外部证据、调用工具、利用记忆与规划能力进行多智能体协作，从而在决策前补充必要信息。该框架以评价导向的检查（evaluation-directed checks）为核心，涵盖结构化工作流与基于观察的控制策略，并系统梳理了工具集成、记忆管理、规划与多智能体协同等设计维度，为可信评估提供可扩展、可解释的证据链支撑。实验对比表明，现有代理型评判者表现不均，凸显了发展专用代理评估体系的必要性，同时指出成本、可靠性、安全性与治理等挑战仍制约其规模化可信部署。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18067
作者: Runyang You,Hongru Cai,Caiqi Zhang,Qiancheng Xu,Meng Liu,Tiezheng Yu,Yongqi Li,Wenjie Li
机构: The Hong Kong Polytechnic University(香港理工大学); University of Cambridge(剑桥大学); Shandong University(山东大学); Huawei Technologies(华为技术)
类目: Computation and Language (cs.CL)
备注: Project page: this https URL

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Artificial intelligence has progressed from task-specific systems toward general models spanning perception, generation, reasoning, and autonomous interaction, making reliable evaluation essential for comparison, deployment, and post-training feedback. At scale, modern evaluation pipelines increasingly rely on LLM-as-a-Judge to produce scores, preferences, and critiques; in its fixed-context setting, the judge relies on the supplied evaluand and evaluation materials. Yet a reliable verdict may require evidence beyond the supplied evaluand and context—either because critical environmental states remain unrecorded in the static execution log, or because controversial factual claims require external verification. Passive judges cannot obtain that missing evidence before deciding, especially when evaluating agent trajectories, executable artifacts, or domain-specific reports. Recent work therefore develops active evaluators that seek missing evidence and update their grounds for judgment before deciding. We bring these advances together through Agent-as-a-Judge, focusing on how evaluators construct and control checking processes within the broader LLM-based judging landscape. We define the scope through evaluation-directed checks and the use of their results, covering both structured workflows and observation-driven control. We organize the design space by multi-agent collaboration, tool integration, memory, and planning, and show how these mechanisms supply the checks and evidence on which a verdict rests. We consolidate published scores and reproduce missing entries for key agentic judges; the comparison reveals uneven agentic gains and motivates dedicated agentic evaluation. We conclude by characterizing application demands and open challenges in cost, reliability, safety, and governance that condition trusted deployment of agentic judges at scale.

[NLP-22] FEND: Far-End Detection in Outbound Voice AI Calls EACL2027

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决语音人工智能（Voice AI）代理在拨出通话时无法准确识别远端接听方类型的问题。传统自动应答机检测（AMD）仅区分“人类”与“机器”，但这种二值标签不足以指导语音AI采取差异化行为——例如，向语音信箱留言、导航交互式语音应答（IVR）系统或与另一AI系统进行简短交互，均需不同处理策略。研究发现，在实际部署中，48.1%为真人接听，44.6%为语音信箱，6.9%为其他AI系统，仅有0.4%为IVR系统，表明现有分类方式存在显著局限性。为此，作者提出FEND框架，通过分析远端音频及其流式转录文本，对每种自动化类别（语音信箱、IVR、AI接听方）分别运行独立检测器，并以“人类”作为默认类别，可在20秒内输出单一标签。在公开基准测试中，该框架对语音信箱的检测达到100%准确率且无误判真人，而传统Asterisk AMD系统误判率高达30%~66%。针对AI接听方，声学特征在不同录音条件下不具可迁移性，但语速时间特征具有强鲁棒性：合成语音词间停顿显著更短，使模型在区分AI与真人时获得0.952的ROC-AUC。在生产流量上，该框架将真人与任意自动化远端的区分F1得分提升至0.979，且仅有2.9%的真人被错误标记为自动化，这一误差率是影响语音AI决策最关键的指标。核心解决方案在于结合多模态输入（音频+转录文本）、针对不同自动化类别设计专用检测器，并利用语义时间特征实现跨场景的高精度识别。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18050
作者: Abhishek Kumar,Suraj Agrawal,Aashraya Sachdeva
机构: Observe.AI(印度)
类目: Computation and Language (cs.CL); Audio and Speech Processing (eess.AS)
备注: Submitted to EACL 2027

点击查看摘要

Abstract:When a conversational Voice AI agent places an outbound call, the far end may be a live person, a voicemail greeting, an interactive voice response (IVR) system, or increasingly another AI system answering on the callee’s behalf. Standard Answering Machine Detection (AMD) reports only whether a human or a machine picked up, a design meant for human call centres, where one machine is as uninteresting as another. That single bit does not tell a Voice AI agent what to do, since leaving a voicemail message, navigating an IVR and holding a short exchange with another AI are three different actions and a two-valued label cannot distinguish them. In 1,000 randomly sampled calls from our own outbound deployment, a live person answered 48.1%, a voicemail system 44.6%, an AI 6.9% and an IVR 0.4%. Our proposed framework, FEND, reads the far end’s audio and its streaming transcript and runs one detector for each automated class (voicemail, IVR, AI callee), with human the default when nothing fires, returning a single label within 20 seconds. On a public benchmark the voicemail components reach 100% accuracy without ever misclassifying a live human, where Asterisk’s AMD misclassifies 30 to 66% of them. For the AI callee, acoustic features do not transfer across recording conditions, but speech timing does, separating AI from human at 0.952 ROC-AUC, because synthesised speech leaves far shorter gaps between words. On production traffic, separating a live person from any automated far end reaches F1 0.979, and only 2.9% of live people are given an automated label, the error that matters most for an agent acting on the result.

[NLP-23] PerfArena: Ranking LLM s by Code Performance

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决当前大型语言模型（Large Language Models, LLMs）代码生成评估体系中长期存在的多维度评价缺失问题，即现有基准测试普遍仅关注代码正确性，而忽视了生成代码在实际应用中的运行效率与资源消耗。这种单一维度的评估无法全面反映代码在生产环境中的适用性，尤其在对时延、内存占用和能耗敏感的场景下。为此，论文提出PERFARENA——一个开源、可复现的评测平台，通过在类Unix系统上执行各LLM生成的代码，综合评估其正确性、延迟（latency）、峰值内存（peak memory）及能耗（energy consumption），实现跨语言、多任务的性能综合排名。该方案的关键在于其模块化架构设计：支持可插拔的正确性校验器（correctness oracles）、性能评估器（performance oracles）、性能分析工具（profilers）以及代码生成工作流，使得新评测后端与测量工具可无缝集成而不需修改整体流程。实验基于两个竞赛数据集——涵盖10个计算密集型任务的CLBGComp和包含99道LeetCode题目的LeetCodeComp——对13个主流LLM进行了评估，共收集9,455个已验证且量化测量的解决方案。结果表明，got-6-astra是唯一在延迟、能耗和内存使用方面均显著优于其他所有模型的LLM，分别在LeetCodeComp和CLBGComp中达到99.7%和100%的正确率，并展现出卓越的性能表现。所有评测结果与代码均公开可用，为开发者按语言和性能指标选择合适的LLM提供了持续更新的权威参考，超越了传统仅依赖正确率或价格的选型方式。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18030
作者: Rui Rua,Hailemariam Mersha,Joao Paulo Fernandes,Karim Ali
机构: New York University Abu Dhabi (纽约大学阿布扎比分校); Universidade do Algarve (阿尔加维大学)
类目: Performance (cs.PF); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Software Engineering (cs.SE)
备注:

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Industry now relies on LLMs to write a large share of production code. Although that code is mostly, but not always, correct, correctness alone does not determine whether it is fit for repeated and extensive use, which depends on what the code costs in time, memory and energy. Most code-generation benchmarks nonetheless evaluate correctness in isolation, and most performance-oriented benchmarks cover few languages and rarely measure energy consumption. To address this gap, we present PERFARENA, an open-source, reproducible arena that ranks LLMs by the performance of their generated code across multiple languages and competitions. PERFARENA executes the solutions of each LLM on a Unix-like host and reports correctness together with latency, peak memory, and energy consumption. Correctness oracles, performance oracles, profilers, and code-generation workflows are pluggable in PERFARENA, enabling the integration of new judging backends and measurement tools without changing the pipeline. We evaluate PERFARENA on two competitions: CLBGComp, 10 compute-intensive benchmarks from the Computer Language Benchmarks Game, and LeetCodeComp, 99 LeetCode problems whose accepted solutions differ measurably in latency. Currently, PERFARENA holds 9,455 correct and measured solutions from 13 state-of-the-art LLMs across 7 programming languages. Across both competitions, got-6-astra is the only LLM that no other LLM beats significantly in latency, energy consumption, or peak memory, solving 99.7% of LeetCodeComp and 100% of CLBGComp. All PERFARENA leaderboards and measured solutions are publicly available, providing a continuously updated reference that helps developers choose an LLM per language and metric, instead of relying only on correctness rates or pricing.

[NLP-24] oward Universal Translatability: On the Statistical Ontology of Generative AI

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决生成式人工智能（Generative AI）在构建“世界模型”过程中所隐含的哲学与实践困境，即其对“翻译功能”的理想化建构如何成为技术范式的核心驱动力。其核心问题在于：当前生成式AI追求的跨模态一致性、物理具身性与多模态对齐，实质上将模型塑造成一种能够实现语言、视觉与物理表征空间间无损对应关系的“通用翻译机器”，而这一理想背后依赖于一种被作者称为“统计本体论”（statistical ontology）的形而上学预设——即认为现实具有可计算的底层统计结构，可通过海量数据与建模还原，而非通过具身的人类阐释与劳动来建构。论文的关键解决方案在于揭示这一预设的历史根源，追溯其自1949年沃伦·韦弗（Warren Weaver）关于机器翻译的备忘录以来，如何从“代码解密”演变为概率分布对齐的技术逻辑，并批判性地对比翻译研究（Translation Studies）中强调语义模糊性、权力不对称性与译者位置性的另类路径，从而指出生成式AI的统计本体论本质上是前康德式的、非批判性的，遮蔽了使机器可译性得以实现的深层社会技术基础设施与人类劳动。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17963
作者: Katia Schwerzmann
机构: SFB Virtuelle Lebenswelten; Ruhr-Universität Bochum (鲁尔大学), Germany
类目: Computation and Language (cs.CL)
备注:

点击查看摘要

Abstract:This paper argues that the problem of translation functions both as a point of origin and as a normative horizon for contemporary generative AI. In current discourse around ‘‘world models,’’ their attainment is framed as the achievement of internal consistency, cross-modal alignment, and physical grounding. Taken together, these demands converge on an ideal of the model as a universal translation machine capable of establishing lossless correspondences across linguistic, visual, and physical representation spaces. I show that this ideal presupposes a metaphysical commitment that I call a statistical ontology: the belief that reality has an underlying, computable statistical structure that can be recovered through sufficient data and modeling, rather than being shaped and accessed through situated human interpretation and labor. Tracing this commitment back to Warren Weaver’s 1949 Memorandum on machine translation, I outline how translation was historically recast as code deciphering and, later, as the alignment of probability distributions across structured outputs. Finally, I contrast this paradigm with approaches in Translation Studies that foreground opacity, power asymmetries, and the translator’s positionality. I argue that generative AI’s statistical ontology is pre-Kantian, pre-critical and obscures the infrastructures and human labor that make machine translatability—as alignment—possible in the first place.

Abstract:Web agents powered by large language and vision-language models are increasingly applied to realistic browser work that spans heterogeneous applications, multimodal content, and stateful workflows. However, existing reproducible web-agent benchmarks cover only a small number of web applications drawn from a few software categories, and restrict modality to text and vision. Live benchmarks broaden site coverage but sacrifice reproducibility, since pages and data drift between runs. Moreover, existing benchmarks do not meaningfully evaluate whether agents can understand and use audio and video content embedded within web tasks. The strongest GenericAgent configurations, Gemini 3.1 Pro omni and Gemma4 31B, both achieve 30.3%. Among reproducible, human-curated web agent benchmarks, WebArena-Pro provides the broadest application coverage and the most comprehensive multimodal support to date. The benchmark treats audio and video as core observations alongside text and vision, with dedicated actions for extracting information from each. WebArena-Pro runs each task in isolation and supports reproducible, parallel evaluation. [NLP-26] In the Eye of the Beholder: Human Label Variation in Textual and Visual Entailment

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决视觉蕴含（Visual Entailment, VE）任务中人类标注差异（Human Label Variation, HLV）的特性及其与自然语言蕴含（Natural Language Inference, NLI）任务之间的对比问题。尽管HLV在NLI领域已有广泛研究，但其在VE场景下的表现仍不清晰，尤其是在图像作为前提（premise）时，人类对同一输入的判断如何变化。本文提出VariNLI-VE数据集，通过重新标注原SNLI数据集中一组原本为中性的样本及其对应的视觉版本，并由相同标注者同时评估文本与图像版本，从而构建具有标签、置信度评分和解释的成对数据，共获得2,250条标注。关键发现是：尽管图像可能包含文本未明确表达的信息，理论上可减少标注分歧，但实际结果并未显示总体上的分歧下降——部分实例的分歧降低，而另一些则上升；更值得注意的是，标注者的解释揭示了即使标签一致，其推理逻辑仍存在显著差异。此外，在对视觉-语言模型（Vision-Language Models, VLMs）进行评估时，模型在VE任务上表现出更低的准确率、与人类标签分布的弱一致性以及更高的不确定性，表明当前模型尚未充分捕捉视觉语义理解中的复杂认知差异。因此，该研究的关键在于通过结构化多模态标注数据揭示并量化跨模态的人类认知变异性，为改进模型对人类判断的拟合能力提供依据。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17954
作者: Monica Riedler,Jasmin Orth,Raffaella Bernardi,Barbara Plank
机构: MaiNLP, Center for Information and Language Processing, LMU Munich(慕尼黑大学信息与语言处理中心), Germany; Munich Center for Machine Learning (MCML)(慕尼黑机器学习中心), Munich, Germany; Free University of Bozen-Bolzano(博岑-博尔扎诺自由大学), Italy

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Human Label Variation (HLV) is increasingly recognised as an informative signal that reflects meaningful differences in how people interpret the same input. While HLV has been studied extensively in Natural Language Inference (NLI), much less is known about HLV in Visual Entailment (VE; Xie et al., 2019), where the textual premise is replaced by an image, keeping the hypothesis and label space fixed. We introduce VariNLI-VE, a dataset for studying how HLV changes between paired NLI and VE instances. We re-annotate a subset of originally neutral SNLI (Bowman et al., 2015) instances and their visual counterparts, with the same annotators evaluating both the textual and visual versions. Each annotation includes a label, confidence score, and explanation, yielding 2,250 annotations. Labels and explanations are collected jointly, providing ecologically valid explanations. Because the image may reveal information not stated in the text, one might expect disagreement to decrease in VE. Instead, we find no clear overall reduction: disagreement decreases for some instances while it increases for others. Explanations reveal further variation, as annotators can retain the same label while changing their rationale. When evaluating vision-language models (VLMs) on the paired data, we find lower accuracy, weaker alignment with human label distributions, and generally higher uncertainty in VE than in NLI.

[NLP-27] Can You Trust Your Finance Agent ? Drift Diagnosis and Auditable Intervention on One Shipped Financial Research Agent

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决生成式 AI（Generative AI）在金融领域应用中的可信度与可解释性问题，具体聚焦于：一个未经微调的基准金融代理（finance agent）在实际运行中是否存在性能不稳定、故障责任归属不清以及优化结果是否可信等关键挑战。其解决方案的关键在于构建一套基于可追溯执行轨迹（traces）的诊断与验证框架：首先通过多轮重复实验评估代理性能的稳定性，发现其评分在不同运行间波动显著（最高达17%）；其次采用预定义的分层故障归因分类法，将失败轨迹归因于指令、工具、模型或数据层，结果显示多数问题源于代理指令设计；进一步引入盲评机制，由来自三个大模型家族的四位独立评审者仅凭轨迹判断故障根源，与大语言模型（LLM）分析的一致率达94%，验证了诊断结论的可靠性；最后提出一种基于追踪反馈的优化循环机制，根据成本或准确率目标进行增量式调整，并在事前设定的统计规则下评估改进效果——结果显示，在成本目标下实现0.34倍于基线的显著节省（95%置信区间[0.22, 0.58]），而在准确率目标下的提升则落入噪声范围，无法确认为真实改善。该研究以单个代理和九个测试问题为案例，强调了在评估金融代理时需超越排行榜分数，重视运行稳定性、故障可解释性及优化有效性验证的重要性。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17939
作者: Cosimo Galeone,Anna Ettorre,Ginevra Mariani,Minsu Park,Giuseppe Ettorre,Daniele Ligorio
机构: Alomana(阿尔奥曼纳)

Abstract:Can you trust a finance agent you did not build? We take the reference agent shipped with the Vals Finance Agent Benchmark v2, leave it untuned, and ask four questions a leaderboard score does not answer. Is the score stable? Seven identical runs on 18 questions pass between 105 and 123 of 149 rubric criteria, and the bill varies by up to 17%. When the agent fails, who owns the fix? We classify 20 failed trajectories, from three runs of nine held-out questions, by the layer whose owner would remedy them, under a taxonomy frozen in advance: 13 belong to the agent’s instructions, 3 to its tools, 4 to the model, none to its data. Can that diagnosis be trusted? Four blind readers from three model families, given only the traces, name the same fault as our LLM analyzer in 94% of cases, the rate at which they agree with one another (93%). Can a change be trusted? An optimization loop reads the traces, makes one targeted change per round and keeps it only if it clears a fixed threshold. Under a cost objective its final configuration costs 0.34x the baseline on the held-out questions (95% CI [0.22, 0.58]) at accuracy within noise. Under an accuracy objective it gains 14 points on the development questions and 1.5 on the held-out ones (CI [-5.3, +7.2]). Judged against the measured run-to-run noise, under a rule written before the test was opened, the first is a real saving and the second is not. This is a case study of one agent and nine test questions, not a general result.

[NLP-28] MineralExp: Benchmarking LLM Agents for Experimental Design in Real-World Mineral Exploration

【速读】： 该论文旨在解决地质勘探中自动化地球化学实验设计（automated geochemical experimental design）这一关键上游问题，即在投入高成本的实验室工作前，如何智能决策应开展哪些地球化学实验以验证地质假说。其核心挑战在于：一方面，地球化学实验具有高度的方法多样性和主观性，同一假说可能对应多种矿物与仪器组合，缺乏统一的参考标准；另一方面，实验方案为开放式生成内容，难以用结构化标签进行客观评估。为此，论文提出MineralExp基准，将实验设计重构为基于假说的多标签分类问题，通过构建涵盖五大矿床系统过程（源、运移、沉积、改造、保存）的19类标准化实验类型体系，实现对具体仪器名称和操作细节的抽象化处理，从而生成确定性的多标签目标，同时兼容不同实验协议。该基准包含277个案例及1572条参考实验计划，经领域专家验证，支持基于多标签分类指标与“大语言模型作为裁判”（LLM-as-a-Judge）的双重评估。实验结果表明，当前大语言模型代理虽具备较高召回率，但精度偏低，普遍存在过度提议实验的问题。解决方案的关键在于建立可泛化、可量化评估的标准化实验类型体系与基于多标签分类的评估范式，为未来自动实验设计研究提供可靠基准。

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17931
作者: Bing Wang,Mingyang Wang,Yuanhang Mi,Yue Xu,Kaibo Su,Li’ang Xiao,Chunguo Wu,Changchun Li,Ximing Li,Kun-Feng Qiu,Bo Yang
机构: Jilin University(吉林大学); China University of Geosciences (Beijing)(中国地质大学（北京）)
类目: Computation and Language (cs.CL); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computational Engineering, Finance, and Science (cs.CE)
