[CV-0] BAL: Bidirectional Autoregression in Latent Space for Learning Human Movement Representations NEURIPS2026

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18559
作者: Genki Kinoshita,Ko Nishino
类目: Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)
备注: Accepted to NeurIPS 2026. Project page: this https URL

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Learning semantically meaningful human movement representations from 3D pose sequences is essential for human behavior modeling. Existing self-supervised methods derive supervision from artificial assumptions, e.g., invariance to augmentation within a certain range or recoverability of masked regions at a certain temporal scale, introducing heuristic hyperparameters that make the learned semantics sensitive to their settings. We propose BAL, a self-supervised framework that instead exploits supervision intrinsic to human movement: the natural ordering structure of everyday behavior. BAL autoregressively predicts in a semantically abstract latent space, analogous to how humans anticipate others’ actions rather than their precise joint configurations. Bidirectional autoregressive prediction, both forward and backward in time, fully leverages these ordering constraints while enabling representation extraction using both past and future context at inference. To suppress representation collapse, BAL decomposes each prediction target into a global component capturing sequence-level semantics and a local component capturing context-independent movement. We prove that the local targets retain nontrivial angular diversity unless the representations are completely collapsed, preserving a meaningful training signal. Experiments on large-scale datasets demonstrate that BAL’s representations are highly effective for motion captioning, forecasting, and interpolation, outperforming baselines on most evaluation metrics.

[CV-1] Restoring Diversity in Distilled Image and Video Generators by Probing Activations

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18550
作者: Furkan Horoz,Ozgur Kara,Kaan Oktay,Adil Kaan Akan,Atakan Kara,Anand Bhattad,James Matthew Rehg
类目: Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)
备注:

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Distilling diffusion models into few-step student models substantially reduces sampling latency, but induces severe diversity collapse: samples generated from different random seeds show minimal variation. In this work, we investigate the root cause of this failure by probing the internal representations of student models relative to their teachers. We find that distilled students maintain total activation variance comparable to their teachers, but concentrate it into substantially fewer directions. This directional collapse is localized to an identifiable intermediate block at the initial denoising step. Consequently, diversity collapse stems from a misallocation of activation variance rather than an inherent loss of generative capacity. Guided by this finding, we propose ReDiverse to reinstate the missing variance directions. At training time, ReDiverse-FT fine-tunes a lightweight adapter that regresses student activations onto the teacher’s, reviving the teacher’s feature variance characteristic. At inference time, ReDiverse-TF introduces a training-free mechanism that steers activations along precomputed teacher principal directions. Across image and video distillation benchmarks, ReDiverse consistently restores sample diversity while preserving generation quality, further supported by the user study.

[CV-3] Understanding Contextual Bias in Agricultural CNNs Through Hidden-Neuron Concept Induction

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18530
作者: Abhilekha Dalal,Avishek Das,Samatha Ereshi Akkamahadevi
类目: Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)
备注:

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) can classify images using background-dominated patches that contain little or none of the target object, showing that they exploit contextual information. Previous studies detected this behavior through classification accuracy but say little about what CNN hidden layers represent. We investigate whether Concept Induction can reveal interpretable visual properties associated with hidden-neuron activations on contextual image patches. Using five agricultural datasets with high, moderate, and low contextual bias, we analyze four CNN architectures at three network depths and five patch locations. Quantitative image descriptors of each patch are encoded in a knowledge graph and used as background knowledge for Concept Induction, which derives logical expressions characterizing neuron activation. These expressions are tested on held-out images using the Mann-Whitney U test and Cliff’s delta. Of 48,013 tested neuron-feature pairs, 13,164 (27.4%) were validated. Validation was most frequent at intermediate depths (mean 40.2% at mid layers versus 12.0% at deep layers), and no patch location dominated across datasets. Among the tested descriptors, edge, Gabor, and Tamura texture features had the highest validation rates. Stronger contextual bias did not correspond to higher neuron-feature validation rates: moderately biased Sugarcane had the highest rate (40.5%) and highly biased Cassava the lowest (19.2%). These results suggest that behavioral context dependence and the semantic interpretability of hidden neurons capture different aspects of CNN behavior, and that Concept Induction can complement patch-based bias detection.

[CV-5] Box-conditional Hypothesis Testing for Out-of-Distribution Object Detection

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18519
作者: Donghoon Lee,Jusung Park,Shinjin Kang
类目: Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)
备注: 18 pages, 7 figures, 13 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Detecting out-of-distribution (OOD) objects is essential for ensuring the reliability and safety of object detectors in real-world deployment. We treat the decision on a detected box as a hypothesis test with the null hypothesis that the box covers an object of an in-distribution (ID) class. The null distribution changes with the size of the box and its contact with the image border, and the detector observes both. The test can therefore rank a box only against ID boxes of the same size and border contact, which gives a distribution-free bound on the false-alarm rate for each of them and uncovers the separation that one null distribution hides. The power of the test also rests on the layer it reads. Layers built only for the ID classes have no reason to keep what sets an unknown object apart, so the test reads the features before them. We propose \textbfPLUM, a training-free score that pools the last backbone stage inside the box and ranks its distance to the ID classes among ID boxes of the same size and border contact. On the standard benchmark, PLUM reaches the highest AUROC among the compared methods without external models, with 91.5 and 94.5 on PASCAL-VOC and 95.1 and 97.5 on BDD100K. Code: this https URL

[CV-8] CoPre-MoE: Consolidating and Preserving MoE for Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18417
作者: Tao Hu,Ling-Xiang Zhang,Da-Wei Zhou
类目: Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Machine Learning (cs.LG)
备注:

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Multimodal continual instruction tuning requires multimodal large language models to sequentially acquire new capabilities without catastrophic forgetting. Mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures offer a promising way to mitigate task interference through expert specialization and combination. However, while existing MoE-based methods avoid overwriting shared parameters by allocating separate experts for each task, this progressive expansion incurs severe cross-task redundancy and linear parameter growth. Specifically, experts across tasks often learn overlapping functions alongside task-specific capabilities. For instance, ScienceQA and TextVQA both locate relevant image regions. To curb parameter redundancy without inducing forgetting, we propose Consolidating and Preserving MoE (CoPre-MoE). For each incoming task, CoPre-MoE first learns task-specific MoE to capture specialized capabilities. To eliminate redundancy and reduce parameter growth across tasks, we design a shared-and-private expert architecture. Specifically, we first evaluate functional overlap to selectively consolidate overlapping experts across tasks into a fixed-capacity generic expert pool. We then factorize the remaining task-specific experts into shared projection bases and compact task-specific cores within a private expert pool. To mitigate forgetting, we construct the router by pairing each expert with learnable retrieval keys, where historical keys remain frozen during continual updates while newly added keys are regularized with an orthogonality penalty. At inference, input-dependent routing dynamically combines both pools without test-time task labels. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CoPre-MoE achieves superior performance while substantially reducing parameter overhead.

[CV-11] MaskSCD: Bitemporal Mask Classification for Semantic Change Detection in Remote Sensing Images

类目: Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)
备注:

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Semantic change detection (SCD) identifies where the land surface has changed and which land-cover transition has taken place. Most deep SCD networks predict the change map and the land-cover map of each date with separate per-pixel decoders. These outputs need not agree, so a pixel can be labeled as changed although it has the same class at both dates, and per-pixel decisions fragment objects. We present MaskSCD, which formulates SCD as the mask classification of a bitemporal label-pair image. Each bitemporal segment, a region of constant land-cover class pair, is predicted as one mask shared by both dates together with one class per date, and the change map is derived from the predicted class pair. This formulation keeps the change and semantic maps consistent and the label space linear in the number of classes. A bitemporal masked-attention transformer decoder implements it by reading each segment in both images and relating the two readings, and a bitemporal fusion pyramid provides per-date and change-aware features. MaskSCD is evaluated on SECOND, Landsat-SCD and Hi-UCD, which span 0.1 to 30 m resolution and four to nine land-cover classes, against fourteen recent SCD models re-trained under one protocol. MaskSCD achieves the best separated kappa, semantic change F_1 and transition F_1 on all three datasets and exceeds the strongest competitor by about five points in mean separated kappa. It produces no contradictory change pixels, which account for nearly 30% of the predicted change of some multi-task networks. The code is available at this https URL.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:State-of-the-art novel-view synthesis (NVS) methods commonly require per-scene optimization before rendering, limiting their applicability in live settings. Live or ad-hoc NVS, however, demands low-latency rendering during capture and strong generalization to new scenes. Some previous work tried to address this task by adapting training- and optimization-based methods, trading quality for low latency. We introduce a live NVS approach following traditional image-based rendering that operates on a posed set of depth sensor observations and images. Our method leverages explicit, sparse point-based geometry obtained from active sensors, resulting in a sparsely filled depth buffer that is completed in the (novel) target view’s screen-space. Using this proxy, a posed set of images is projected into the target frame before fusing the resulting observations through a frequency-aware two-band blending strategy. We validate the method’s applicability to heterogeneous sensor setups - including handheld RGB-D cameras, automotive LiDAR, and thermal cameras - as well as challenging scenarios, such as low light captures. Our experiments demonstrate that the approach outperforms prior methods in live reconstruction scenarios, while maintaining competitive performance in conventional NVS benchmarks.

Abstract:Weakly Supervised Video Semantic Segmentation (WS-VSS) aims to learn temporally consistent semantic object masks in videos using only video-level labels. Therefore, existing WS-VSS methods exploit temporal consistency by relying on external priors, such as optical flow. Experiments on the YouTube-Objects v2.2 and WS ^2 datasets demonstrate that MemCAM consistently outperforms the temporal localization and segmentation performance of WS-VSS alternatives, showing that cross-attention can be effectively trained in label-scarce video settings through internal self-supervision.

[CV-15] raCoder: Adaptive Tetrahedral Encoding for Neural Fields

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18267
作者: Daniele Della Pietra,Antonella Rech,Nicola Conci,Nicola Garau

备注: 8 pages, 4 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Implicit and explicit representations of geometry are central in computer graphics applications and come with their pros and cons. With surface sampling being faster on explicit representations and spatial queries being faster on implicit representations, finding efficient conversions between them is a highly desirable property. We present TetraCoder, a single resolution hash-free adaptive tetrahedral encoding that links an explicit tetrahedral spatial encoder with an underlying implicit signed distance field (SDF). The resulting hybrid implicit-explicit space is continuous piecewise affine (CPWA) by design, allowing to obtain an exact correspondence between the implicit SDF and the explicit analytic mesh. TetraCoder’s design comes with additional perks, in particular: (a) it scales better than multi-resolution encoding methods due to its single resolution optimizable grid, (b) unlike hash-based methods, it is not affected by collisions, © it enables fast tetrahedral ray-tracing, geometry and appearance editing, directly through its adaptive tetrahedral scaffold. We demonstrate the versatility of TetraCoder by showing an improvement over state-of-the-art methods on multiple tasks: 2D/3D fitting from images and point clouds, 3D novel view synthesis, 3D reconstruction, and geometry/appearance editing.

[CV-16] Structural Self-Teaching for Compositional Generation in Unified Multimodal Models NEURIPS2026

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18261
作者: Jaeyoo Park,Bohyung Han
类目: Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)
备注: NeurIPS 2026

点击查看摘要

Abstract:We propose Structural Self-Teaching (SST), a novel framework that enhances compositional image generation in diffusion-based unified multimodal models (UMMs) by leveraging their internal understanding path as a source of structural supervision. The generation path of UMMs often struggles with dense compositional prompts, leading to missing objects, attribute binding failures, and spatial errors. In contrast, their understanding path localizes the same phrases in images that satisfy these prompts. SST converts this phrase-level grounding into training-time structural signals for the generation path. Specifically, we extract phrase-conditioned support masks from internal attention maps derived from the understanding path and employ them through two mechanisms. Phrase-local contrastive alignment uses these masks as pooling regions and aligns each generated phrase region with the same region of the target image. A structural scaffold builds a phrase-level layout from the same masks and modulates an intermediate spatial state of the generation path during training. The scaffold is phased out during training and removed at inference. SST requires no external structural annotations for training and no additional input at test time. Extensive experiments across multiple UMMs demonstrate that SST improves compositional fidelity over LoRA fine-tuning on the same data, with no inference-time cost. [CV-17] Deep Bundle Adjustment

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18256

Abstract:We present \textbfDeepBA (\textbfDeep \textbfBundle \textbfAdjustment), a graph neural network (GNN) that recovers camera poses, intrinsics, and scene structure from 2D matches across an arbitrary number of views. Rather than solving a non-linear least-squares problem per scene, DeepBA amortizes bundle adjustment into a learned graph network that iteratively refines its estimate from geometric residuals. [CV-18] Pose-based Neural Clothing for Animated Characters SIGGRAPH

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18253
作者: Julian N. Heidenreich,Vinicius C. Azevedo,Jakob Buhmann,Lento Manickathan,Arnold Moon,Paul Kanyuk,Amit H. Bermano,Jingwei Tang
类目: Graphics (cs.GR); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)
备注: 19 pages, 18 figures, 5 tables. Includes appendix. Accepted to the ACM SIGGRAPH / Eurographics Symposium on Computer Animation (SCA 2026); to appear in Computer Graphics Forum

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Traditional garment simulation for animated characters is a time-consuming bottleneck for artists due to slow simulations and tedious parameter this http URL data-driven methods offer a powerful alternative, capable of learning from a dataset of simulated data to generate high-quality deformations. A lightweight network design allows data-driven systems to achieve a real-time inference speed. This significantly improves the artist workflow, as they can interactively pose a character and see high-quality folds and wrinkles at animation time, thereby allowing to make creative decisions ahead of time. However, existing neural clothing methods have presented appealing results on datasets based on realistic human models (\eg SMPL), but when trained on animated character datasets, they struggle to generalize to unseen poses. In this paper, we aim at designing neural networks and training strategies to generalize better to unseen poses in real-time to enable the interactive posing applications. We focus on a production-oriented setting where the model is trained for a single garment in a quasi-static setup. Technically, we leverage a smooth, expressive latent space by first training a Variational Auto-Encoder (VAE). We then progressively train the VAE encoder to transition from reconstruction to pose-driven prediction. To guide this prediction, we condition the network on both low-dimensional pose parameters and character body surface displacements. Finally, we introduce a novel local gradient histogram loss that mitigates the over-smoothing effect of reconstruction losses. Extensive evaluations on production-level assets and public datasets demonstrate that our method produces sharp, realistic wrinkles, smooth large-scale drape, and temporal coherence in real-time, offering a robust and easily integrable solution for interactive posing workflows.

[CV-19] Lens: Training-Free Semantic Search for Cross-Tile Object and Scene Retrieval in Satellite Imagery

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18251
作者: Ata Akbari Asanjan,Anit Upadhyaya,Jeremy Kravitz,Subash Yeggina
类目: Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)
备注:

点击查看摘要

Abstract:We developed Lens, a training-free system for semantic object and scene search across large satellite mosaics that requires no detector trained for the class of interest. Lens represents each tile with a frozen, self-supervised vision transformer (DINOv3), removes the positional bias in its dense features by projecting them onto the subspace orthogonal to a positional basis estimated once from a constant image, and centers the debiased features on the mean feature computed across the whole area before comparison. Lens forms a query by averaging the centered features of a small neighborhood of patches around a chosen point, or of an entire tile for scene-level search, and matches it against the index by cosine similarity. It stores the centered patch features in a byte-quantized similarity index that supports real-time range search over a mosaic of thousands of patches, and a coarser whole-tile embedding index for scene-level retrieval. A guided upsampling network (AnyUp) runs once per tile to recover pixel-accurate boundaries beyond the native feature grid, and the result is cached so later queries reuse it. We evaluated Lens on the 5,128-tile DFC2020 test set and ran it as an interactive tool on five Sentinel-2 areas spanning agricultural, industrial, and desert terrain; the pipeline is not tied to a specific sensor. On DFC2020 a single click reaches 0.09 to 0.80 pixel F1 across eight land-cover classes at the per-class best threshold, with a mean F1 of 0.39, and it beats a pairwise INSID3 baseline on six of the eight classes at roughly four hundred times lower query cost. Indexing 1.31 million patches takes 169 s on one CPU thread, and a click returns a rendered overlay in 6 to 10 ms.

[CV-20] SCAR: Bridging Coarse Retrieval and Fine Localization through Candidate Reranking

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18240
作者: Shuhan Dong,Tianyi Shang,Zhenyu Li
类目: Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)
备注:

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Natural-language point-cloud localization typically retrieves candidate map cells and then predicts a position inside each candidate, yet the final output is still determined by retrieval order. A retrieval score measures region-description relevance, not whether the coordinates a region yields are accurate: a relevant region may produce a displaced prediction, and a lower-ranked region may contain a successful one. We study the final selection problem after the candidate pool and Fine predictions are fixed—how to choose the coordinate most likely to localize successfully, and under which conditions the gain holds. We propose SCAR (Spatial Consistency-Aware Reranking). SCAR treats each cell–Fine-coordinate pair as a localization hypothesis, interprets directional relations as functions of position, and reads spatial support at the backbone’s actual Fine point (Fine-conditioned Evidence Readout). It organizes this readout with center support, semantic presence, retrieval score, and geometric offsets into a 23-D representation, trains a shared scorer with localization-success labels, and at inference reranks existing hypotheses and returns their original coordinates. Experiments cover four public backbones and a corrected Des4Pos reimplementation across five domains. Excluding one protocol-reference configuration, exploratory TEST point estimates on the remaining 24 configurations show Top-1@5,m above Original on 23 and Cell R@1 above Original on all 24. On development-set VAL, the Fine readout improves Top-1@5,m by 5.77 percentage points (pp) over a center surrogate on corrected Des4Pos*KITTI, and the complete scoring scheme improves it by 13.98,pp over FSR on MambaPlace \times nuScenesFull; the latter measures the complete scheme, not learned ranking alone.

[CV-21] SemRAG : Semantic-Guided Sentence-Level Retrieval-Augmented Sign Language Production

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18189
作者: Xiao Liu,Shiwei Gan,Yafeng Yin,Peishuo Liu,Bowen Guo,Kuizhuang Liu,Cong Wang,Lei Xie
类目: Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)
备注:

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Sign language production (SLP) aims to generate sign pose sequences that faithfully express spoken-language text while preserving the rhythm and coordination patterns of signing. Common SLP methods learn text-to-pose mappings from training data but often suffer from semantic inaccuracies in long or complex sentences. Retrieval augmentation offers a way to introduce realistic signing priors to provide more explicit guidance for text-to-pose generation. However, existing retrieval-based SLP methods typically operate at the gloss or word level, relying on gloss-pose dictionaries or isolated sign segments that require costly annotations and fail to preserve continuous sentence-level dynamics. To bridge this gap, we propose SemRAG, a semantic-guided sentence-level retrieval-augmented framework that retrieves full-sentence signing templates as priors without constructing gloss/word-level sign dictionaries. While such templates preserve local motion patterns and global rhythm, they also introduce a semantic mismatch problem: irrelevant motions in the retrieved sentence may interfere with the target semantics. To address this issue, we introduce an SLT-guided template semantic localization module that identifies target-relevant temporal regions within the retrieved template while suppressing irrelevant signing content. To adaptively incorporate the template features into the generator, we further introduce layer-specific learnable gates that regulate their contribution across generator layers. Extensive experiments show that SemRAG improves semantic consistency and motion quality over baselines under both back-translation and motion accuracy metrics.

[CV-22] STaG: Human-Annotation-Free Unsupervised Domain Adaptation for Text-Based Person Retrieval

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18172
作者: Ji Yucheng,Bai Yang,Li Yang,Zhou Xinyu,Cao Min
类目: Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)
备注:

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Real-world deployment of Text-Based Person Retrieval (TBPR) is bottlenecked by the absence of textual annotations in new domains. Existing Unsupervised Domain Adaptation (UDA-TBPR) methods still assume access to target-domain human-written annotations (albeit unpaired with images), limiting their practicality. We consider a more practical setting: human-annotation-free UDA-TBPR, with labeled image-text data in the source domain and annotation-free, unlabeled images in the target domain. This setting introduces two core challenges: the absence of cross-modal alignment in the target domain, and visual domain shifts from source to target. To tackle these, we propose STaG, a dual-path framework comprising Style-Agnostic Text Alignment (SATA) and Heterogeneous Graph Memory Alignment (HGMA). Specifically, SATA leverages source-domain data to decouple identity-discriminative semantics from machine-generated stylistic artifacts, establishing robust cross-modal alignment for the target domain. Meanwhile, HGMA exploits cross-domain semantic neighborhoods via a memory-based graph to learn domain-invariant visual features. Ultimately, STaG achieves state-of-the-art performance across three benchmarks under this realistic setting.

Abstract:Affine correspondences (ACs) enable robust and efficient solvers for multiple-view geometry by constraining the local geometry of point correspondences (PCs). [CV-24] DRA-HTR: Disentangling Representation and Adaptation for Handwritten Text Recognition

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18143

类目: Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)
备注:

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Handwritten text recognition (HTR), particularly when applied to historical documents, remains challenging due to the scarcity of annotated data and the substantial domain shifts caused by variations in handwriting styles, document degradation, acquisition conditions, and linguistic characteristics. While large amounts of unlabeled target-domain data are often available, effectively exploiting them requires adaptation strategies tailored to the specific requirements of HTR. We propose DRA-HTR, an unsupervised generative domain adaptation pipeline specifically designed for HTR and built upon the DRANet framework. The proposed approach combines source-domain pretraining, unsupervised adaptation to the visual characteristics of the target domain, relying on the generation of target-style source samples for task-specific training. We further investigate the integration of post-HTR correction to complement visual adaptation with language adaptation, relying on large language models, which we introduce as DRAL-HTR. The proposed framework is evaluated across seven HTR datasets spanning four languages, using a single set of hyperparameters for all source-target combinations. The recognition model achieves state-of-the-art performance on four datasets and competitive results on the remaining three. In addition, the proposed DRA-HTR establishes a new state-of-the-art for domain adaptation across all seven datasets and substantially improves overall robustness and training stability compared to the original DRANet framework: it reduces the Character Error Rate (CER) in average by 13.52 percentage points, and the average standard deviation decreases from 7.23 to 1.11 percentage points. Finally, post-HTR correction further reduces the CER by 2.16 percentage points.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Text-to-image (T2I) models have made substantial progress in visual realism, aesthetic quality, and instruction following. However, real-world prompts often go beyond explicit visual descriptions and require implicit facts, structured knowledge, and domain-specific commonsense. Existing evaluations mainly focus on explicit prompt-to-image semantic alignment or specific knowledge subdomains, lacking unified evaluation and diagnosis of world-knowledge grounding in T2I models. We introduce KnowVis for dual-view evaluation and diagnosis of world-knowledge grounding in T2I generation. Built on a hierarchical knowledge taxonomy with case-level annotations, KnowVis combines two complementary benchmark views with a structured MLLM-based VQA judge protocol. The Skill-Tree Benchmark supports fine-grained atomic knowledge evaluation, while the Generalized Benchmark examines open-ended knowledge composition and expression. The protocol uses Required VQAs to assess atomic knowledge correctness in the Skill-Tree Benchmark, and combines Required, Optional, and dynamically discovered knowledge expressions with human-judge collaborative evaluation in the Generalized Benchmark. We validate the protocol’s reliability on the Skill-Tree validation split, enabling scalable evaluation and diagnostic error attribution. Experiments show that KnowVis reveals notable gaps in world-knowledge grounding across existing T2I models and provides structured diagnostic signals for knowledge-intensive image generation.

[CV-26] EcoVGGT: Pinned-Key Inference for Efficient and Streamable Feed-Forward 3D Reconstruction from Long Videos

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18099
作者: Hongsen Cao,Shanxin Yuan,Mona Jaber,Ahmed Sayed
类目: Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)
备注:

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Feed-forward multi-view transformers such as VGGT recover the cameras and geometry of a video in one pass, but the cost of their cross-frame attention grows quadratically with the frame count, and their accuracy drops on long videos. Most remedies approximate attention over all frames within a budget or select a few keys by their content. We hypothesize that the keys of a long video can be chosen without looking at its content, and our experiments on VGGT and \pi^3 support it: a few dozen evenly spread frames can serve as the keys of every frame. We present EcoVGGT, a training-free inference framework whose keys form a Pinned Key Set of 31 such frames. A pin pass stores their tokens in a Scene Cache, and the other frames read it in independent batches that reproduce joint inference with these keys exactly, at a constant cost per frame. An Online Stream updates the pinned frames during recording. Six middle global blocks align the frames and form a reusable VGGT module: the Alignment Score reads their attention to flag failures, and Alignment Refocusing reduces them by fine-tuning only these blocks (3.6 GPU hours). EcoVGGT ( \pi^3 ) matches the success rate of \pi^3 on 1,000-frame ScanNet videos at 13.8 times its speed. With refocusing, EcoVGGT (VGGT) raises the success rate of VGGT from 70% to 82% at 8.5 times its speed on 500 frames. EcoVGGT reconstructs 4,000 frames on one GPU, and EcoVGGT ( \pi^3 ) streams 1,000 frames at 18.6 frames per second with the lowest online absolute trajectory error.

Existing Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) models learn general structural representations but do not explicitly capture the anatomical regions affected in AD. [CV-28] Physica: Agent ic Reconstruction of Physically Interactive Scenes

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18078
作者: Ruiqi Shu,Xufeng Zhan,Yuanzhi Liang,Haibin Huang,Chi Zhang,Xuelong Li
类目: Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)
备注: Ongoing Work. Homepage: this https URL

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Direct image-to-video diffusion models entangle pixel synthesis with implicit latent motion, precluding persistent 3D mechanical equilibrium, mid-rollout intervention, and counterfactual force control. Conversely, single-pass programmatic 3D reconstruction from an uncalibrated photograph leaves monocular depth and support clearance underconstrained, causing reconstructed scenes to interpenetrate or collapse under gravity. We present TelePhysica, a closed-loop framework that reconstructs an executable, multi-regime physical world from a single photograph to drive stateful 3D interaction and trajectory-grounded video synthesis. A vision-language coding agent synthesizes separable visual meshes and declarative physical models within a two-stage verification loop that pairs pixel-aligned silhouette critique with a zero-force gravitational equilibrium preflight. The verified world compiles into a unified solver coupling rigid bodies, articulated chains, co-dimensional cloth shells, and 3D tetrahedral elastodynamics, whose simulated rollouts condition pretrained video diffusion models through pixel-aligned structural streams. On a 100-scene single-image benchmark, closed-loop execution raises equilibrium-verified world recovery from 0% in single-pass synthesis to 81% . Controlled constitutive ablations show over an order-of-magnitude larger localized corner displacement and out-of-plane pleating in finite-element cloth than in matched rigid-plate proxies. Conditioning video diffusion on simulated trajectories increases dynamic motion-mask overlap by 2\times\text–5\times and preserves motion direction under counterfactual force reversals where direct image-to-video models fail.

[CV-29] SWAM: Saliency-Weighted Asymmetric Merging for Efficient Vision Transformers

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18057
作者: Utkarsh Kumar Singh,Manish Das,Shiv Ram Dubey,Satish Kumar Singh
类目: Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)
备注:

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Vision Transformers (ViTs) achieve strong performance on image recognition tasks, yet their quadratic self-attention complexity over input tokens creates prohibitive computational costs for practical deployment. Token reduction methods offer a practical solution by progressively reducing the token sequence during the forward pass. We propose Saliency-Weighted Asymmetric Merging (SWAM), a training-based token merging framework that extends the Bipartite Soft Matching (BSM) with two saliency-aware mechanisms. We introduce a two-step matching formulation in which cosine similarity decides candidate pairs, while a multiplicative maximum saliency penalty regulates their selection for merging. We then further use asymmetric exponential saliency-weighted aggregation, which allows more salient tokens to contribute more strongly to the merged representation. A lightweight MLP-based saliency prediction module (75k parameters per stage, 1% of backbone parameters) supervised via attention rollout is trained end-to-end jointly with a DeiT-Small backbone using a composite cross-entropy, knowledge-distillation, and saliency-alignment objective. On CIFAR-100, TinyImageNet, and ImageNet-100, under a conservative schedule that reduces tokens from 196 to 100 patch tokens, with the CLS token retained throughout, 27.5% FLOPs reduction and 1.35 times speedup, SWAM matches or exceeds baseline across the reported results. Under an aggressive schedule (196 to 70 tokens with the CLS token kept throughout, 33.8% FLOPs reduction, and 1.46 times speedup), accuracy gains are achieved on ImageNet-100 (+0.23%) with minimal losses on CIFAR-100 and TinyImageNet.

[CV-30] InfiHall-Bench: A Benchmark for Hallucination Evaluation in Autoregressive Text-to-Image Generation

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18053
作者: Yuxia Qiao,Xinhao Zhong,Junhao Li,Yi Sun,Jiaxin Hong,Shuoyang Sun,Bin Chen
类目: Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)
备注:

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Hallucination benchmarks for text-to-image generation are predominantly designed around diffusion models, limiting their diagnostic value for visual autoregressive (VAR) models and the development of mitigation methods. We identify two limitations in existing prompt construction paradigms: many prompts are no longer challenging for modern generators, while category-based construction relies on recurring object-attribute-relation templates and rarely tests interactions among multiple visual requirements within a single image. We introduce InfiHall-Bench, a controlled benchmark of 411 prompts constructed by systematically composing five foundational and visually verifiable skills: attribute binding, spatial relation, action relation, part consistency, and scene consistency. Each prompt covers 4.05 annotated skills on average, enabling the evaluation of hallucinations under diverse combinations of visual requirements. Using this benchmark, we find that hallucinations in VAR models exhibit a scale-wise persistence pattern: semantic errors can emerge at different autoregressive scales and are rarely corrected by subsequent fine-scale steps. Motivated by this observation, we propose Confidence-Masked Hallucination Mitigation (CMHM), which estimates the prediction confidence of target visual tokens at each autoregressive scale during teacher-forced fine-tuning. CMHM downweights reliably predicted tokens, allowing optimization to focus on uncertain predictions across scales. Experiments show that InfiHall-Bench distinguishes models that existing compositional benchmarks rate as comparable, while CMHM consistently outperforms uniform LoRA fine-tuning for VAR-based text-to-image generation. [CV-31] Multi-level latent-diffusion adaptation for cross-region point cloud segmentation of ancient terraces

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18033
作者: Sina Mohammadi,Roderik Lindenbergh,Javier Ruiz-Pérez,Marco Madella,Sebastian Fajardo
类目: Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)
备注: 33 pages, 6 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Automated lidar feature detection from lidar point clouds can support archaeological surveys, but models trained in one landscape may transfer poorly to another because of differences in terrain, vegetation, acquisition characteristics, and feature morphology. We address this problem for cross-region detection of ancient terraces using transductive unsupervised domain adaptation that exploits an unlabelled target survey. We propose MELD (Multi-level Embedding-space Latent Diffusion), which trains separate diffusion denoisers on target-domain representations at four hierarchical PointNet++ feature levels. These target distribution priors are then used to refine source representations during supervised training and target representations during inference, allowing adaptation to act directly on the multi-scale features consumed by the segmentation decoder. We additionally investigate local geometric descriptors derived from K-nearest-neighbour neighbourhoods, including surface normals, slope, and aspect. MELD is evaluated bidirectionally between the Teyuna and Miraflores lidar surveys in the Sierra Nevada de Santa Marta, Colombia, against a source-only model and nine configurations of established domain adaptation approaches. MELD raises F1 over the source-only model from 0.397 to 0.459 for Teyuna-to-Miraflores transfer and from 0.557 to 0.594 in the reverse direction, achieving the highest F1 among the evaluated adaptation methods in both directions. Adding local geometry further raises F1 to 0.542 and 0.606, respectively. Multi-level ablation shows that refining all four PointNet++ levels provides the strongest performance in both directions. These results show that explicit modelling of target feature distributions in hierarchical point cloud feature space, complemented by local geometry, provides an effective route to cross-region archaeological mapping without target labels.

[CV-32] JaRS: Contrast-Free Localization of Memorized Regions in Diffusion Models via Jacobian Row Sums

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18024
作者: Saisuchith Mahajan,Aditey Nandan,Rohit Singh,Nishanth Shetty,Chandra Sekhar Seelamantula
类目: Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)
备注:

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Diffusion models sometimes memorize and reproduce regions of their training images, raising privacy and copyright concerns. We aim to localize these regions. Existing contrast-based methods use the diagonal of the denoiser Jacobian, which reflects the posterior variance of each pixel. However, a small diagonal entry is ambiguous: it signals either memorization or averaging over many neighboring pixels in smooth or predictable regions. To resolve this ambiguity, these methods subtract a reference map, which requires a second evaluation with a null prompt or an earlier checkpoint. Unconditional models have no null prompt, and earlier checkpoints are unavailable in general. We show that Jacobian row sums (JaRS), unlike the diagonal, separate memorization from local averaging without a reference map. Pairing JaRS with an image-level detection score that uses the same statistic at an early denoising step yields JaRS+. Across Stable Diffusion v1.4 and v2.1, Realistic Vision v5.1, and an unconditional diffusion Transformer (DiT), JaRS+ outperforms contrast-based localization methods.

[CV-33] RSSA: Robust Semantic and Spatial Aligner for Collaborative Perception NEURIPS2026

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18023

类目: Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)
备注: Accepted to NeurIPS 2026. 18 pages, 14 figures, 11 tables. Code: this https URL

点击查看摘要

Abstract:V2X collaborative perception improves single-vehicle perception by aggregating multi-view features from collaborative agents. However, existing methods typically rely on simple spatial warping to align the collaborative features, which can cause semantic misalignment during fusion. Meanwhile, these deterministic warping-based methods are highly sensitive to the positional noises. In this paper, we propose a plug-in module called Robust Semantic and Spatial Aligner (RSSA), which performs semantic and probabilistic spatial transformations for robust feature alignment. Specifically, a Semantic Transformation (SemT) block modulates feature channels based on the observed relative pose to correct semantic inconsistencies. Subsequently, a Probabilistic Spatial Transformation (P-SpaT) block performs probabilistic warping by sampling from the Gaussian-modeled relative pose space. A Relative Transformation Augmentation (RT-Aug) strategy is further introduced to augment the relative poses and facilitate the training of the RSSA. Extensive experiments on both simulated and real-world datasets demonstrate that RSSA improves the state-of-the-art methods by a large margin under the configuration of both clean and noisy positions.

[CV-34] RecurIR: Learning When and How to Revise Restoration Policies via Recursive Self-Improvement

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18006
作者: Hu Gao,Haoran Zhang,Yulong Chen,Lizhuang Ma
类目: Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)
备注:

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Agent-based image restoration has achieved promising performance by coordinating restoration tools to handle heterogeneous and coupled degradations. However, existing agents often improve their decisions through accumulated successful and failed executions, without explicitly identifying the conditions under which learned restoration strategies remain effective. Consequently, changes in degradation severity, spatial coverage, or interactions may invalidate previous decisions, while providing limited guidance on how these decisions should be revised. In this paper, we propose \textbfRecurIR, a recursive self-improvement framework that enables restoration agents to learn when and how to revise their decision rules. Specifically, we introduce a policy-hypothesis discovery mechanism to identify testable, condition-dependent rules through matched tool executions. We then design a rule-falsification-driven task evolution mechanism to generate targeted degradation variations, examine the validity of learned rules, and identify their applicability boundaries. Based on the resulting evidence, we develop a change-conditioned policy revision mechanism that learns how variations in degradation conditions should modify tool selection, execution order, operation strength, and processing regions. We further incorporate independent verification to retain effective revisions and use the resulting feedback to guide subsequent hypothesis discovery and task evolution. Through this recursive process, RecurIR progressively refines its restoration policies as degradation conditions change. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework.

[CV-35] From Images to Grounded Edits: A Real-World Benchmark for Chinese Grammatical Error Correction in Handwritten Essays

Abstract:Chinese Grammatical Error Correction (CGEC) is typically formulated as a text-to-text problem. While suitable for digital texts, this formulation falls short in real-world K-12 education, where AI-assisted corrections are expected directly on essay images, which are primarily handwritten and exhibit many irregularities. To bridge this gap, we introduce Grounded Chinese Grammatical Error Correction (G-CGEC), an image-to-grounded-edit task requiring systems to predict both corrections and their positions on essay images. We propose G-CGEC-Eval, a benchmark comprising real-world images of K-12 Chinese essays with fine-grained annotations. Results show that even top-tier proprietary models perform surprisingly poorly in the single-pass setting, while pipelining and agentic harnessing yield meaningful improvements. Notably, an error-type-based multi-agent workflow achieves the best performance, establishing it as the strongest baseline for future G-CGEC research. Through ablations and fine-grained analysis, we find the task remains far from solved, with linguistic error discovery and character-level grounding as the primary bottlenecks. This underscores the need for models that better integrate visual perception with linguistic reasoning and visual grounding.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Arbitrary-scale light field spatial super-resolution reconstructs high-resolution views at any requested scale from low-resolution multi-view observations. Gaussian primitives enable continuous reconstruction, but generating separate primitive sets for each view redundantly encodes shared scene structure across views and causes the primitive count grow linearly with the number of views. To avoid such redundancy, we propose MacroGS, a feed-forward decoder that jointly predicts a single shared Gaussian set per macro pixel rather than generating separate sets for each view. Disparity-dependent replacement and angle-conditioned appearance allow each set to be reused across all output views, keeping the predicted primitive count independent of the number of views at each requested scale. The decoder iteratively predicts per-primitive disparities, retrieves the corresponding multi-view features, and updates the primitive states. Gaussian splatting and residual refinement then reconstruct the output light field. Despite being learned without geometric supervision, the predicted disparities strongly correlate with underlying scene geometry. Experiments across five benchmarks demonstrate that MacroGS integrates effectively with diverse light field encoders, improving reconstruction quality at fixed scales while achieving leading performance across most evaluated arbitrary-scale settings. The decoder also achieves speedups of approximately 2 - 10\times over state-of-the-art implicit light field decoders and 10 - 18\times over the view-wise Gaussian baseline.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Character classification and handwritten text recognition, particularly in specialized tasks, often suffer from limited labeled data, while a large amount of unlabeled data remains unused. To address this challenge, we propose a novel unsupervised domain adaptation-based pipeline to reduce the distribution gap between source and target domains. Our pipeline follows three main steps. In the first step, we pretrained an expert model on source data, to learn a strong general representation. In the second step, we perform unsupervised domain adaptation to leverage unlabeled target data, adapting the model to the specific characteristics of the target domain. In the third step, we trained the expert model on source data in the target style. Abstract:Attention is essential for modeling spatio-temporal dependencies in video diffusion transformers, but its quadratic cost makes it a major inference bottleneck. However, accuracy and execution favor different granularities: each query attends to its own key tokens, whereas GPU kernels work on tiles. [CV-43] DiffusionSSD: Achieving Extreme Visual Compression with Dual-stage Diffusion and Cross-level Near-Data Processing

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17908
作者: Kuai Yu,Wen Li,Shengwen Liang,Jie Zhang,Ying Wang,Huawei Li,Xin Yao,Renhai Chen,Gong Zhang

Abstract:Growing visual data overwhelms storage on resource-constrained edge devices, making compression essential. Traditional codecs offer inadequate rate-distortion performance, motivating the exploration of diffusion codecs. Diffusion codecs face two limitations: (1) At the algorithm level, diffusion decoders preserve perceptual quality under extreme compression, but require substantial computation. (2) At the architecture level, in NPU-centered devices, neural codecs occupy the NPU, interfering with other AI applications. Meanwhile, the complex codec pipeline incurs long I/O paths, leading to additional overhead and energy cost. Specifically, (1) at algorithm level, we present Dual-stage Diffusion, a lightweight solution tailored for diffusion codecs that employs patchification and single-step diffusion, reducing computation cost while preserving perceptual quality; (2) at architecture level, we introduce a Cross-level Near Data Processing architecture that integrates the proposed computational universal flash storage (cUFS) and LPDDR-PIM, enabling codecs to be entirely offloaded to data side while reducing visual-data movement and avoiding SoC redesign; (3) at system level, combining both the algorithm and the underlying architecture, we propose a Patch-Workload co-aware scheduling scheme to enable more efficient system-level execution. Experiments show that Dual-stage Diffusion maintains comparable perceptual quality while reducing computation cost by up to 92.7%. Compared with NPU+DRAM and near-data-processing design such as SmartSSD, cUFS+PIM improves coding throughput by 2.32 times and 2.91 times on average, respectively. [CV-45] Zoom In Scale Out: Metric Depth Models Mistake Digital Zoom for Moving Closer

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17903
作者: Rabeya Noor,Khandoker Nosiba Arifin
类目: Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)
备注: 22 pages including supplementary material. Code: this https URL

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Many new AI models estimate how far away things are, in metres, from a single photo. To do this they must also work out what camera took the photo. We check whether they really do with a simple test: digital zoom, that is, cropping a photo and enlarging the crop back to full size. Zooming moves nothing in the scene, so a correct model should give the same distance for every point still in the picture. The test is free, exact and needs no labels. Two numbers describe a model’s reaction: its depth changes like s^-\alpha (ideal \alpha=0 ) and its focal-length estimate like s^\beta (ideal \beta=1 ), for zoom factor s . On 800 images from KITTI, NYUv2 and DIODE, the widely used Depth Anything V2 has \alpha=0.95 : it treats a zoomed photo almost as if the camera had moved closer, because it judges distance mainly from how large things look. Models that also predict the camera remove only part of this error ( \alpha\approx0.31 - 0.36 ), because their focal estimate grows too little. Given the true camera, Depth Pro and DA3-Nested keep their depth ( \alpha\approx0 ), so the error lies in their camera estimate. The reason: a zoomed photo is, pixel for pixel, a normal photo of a scene squashed in depth, so size alone cannot tell the two apart, and a focal estimator trained by regression stays close to the cameras it saw in training. Undoing this needs no labels or training and makes Depth Pro pass the test ( \alpha=0.04 ) at almost no cost on normal photos. Controls, including a real optical zoom lens, rule out blur and resizing artefacts. Finally, label-free fine-tuning for self-consistency lowers \alpha to 0.05 on KITTI and 0.07 on NYUv2 once a “blurry means zoomed” shortcut is removed; we report what this costs on normal and unseen photos.

[CV-50] Speed-Variation Distillation for Generative Modeling ECCV

点击查看摘要

Abstract:While diffusion models excel at image generation, their denoising trajectories pass through corrupted intermediate states far from the image manifold. We ask whether generation can instead proceed through recognizable images throughout the entire trajectory. We introduce a generative framework built around a variation-reducing degradation process that contracts diverse images toward a shared prototype while preserving recognizable intermediate states. [CV-55] Beyond Endpoints: Bézier Paths for Vision Foundation Model Adaptation

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17825
作者: Chenzhi Zheng,Xi Xiao,Chih-Ting Liao,Qizhen Lan,Jingwen Lin,Tianyang Wang,Hao Xu,Xin Cao
类目: Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)
备注: 47 pages

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Frozen vision foundation models can be adapted by learning a few prompt tokens shared across all images. When prompts are updated as data shift or tasks change, the distribution of image features moves from one endpoint to another. Prior theory asks which endpoints prompts can reach; we ask whether they can travel between them along the shortest path. Because shared prompts compete with each image’s own content for attention, they often cannot. We quantify this restriction as the extra transport cost that shared attention imposes over the shortest path. In an exactly solvable attention model, the reachable features form a cone; unrolling it into a plane gives the exact minimum action for every pair of endpoints and explicit optimal prompts on short arcs. The analysis reveals a sharp threshold in content normalization, which measures how much attention each image’s own tokens absorb: for same-sign endpoints, at most two distinct levels allow prompts to follow the shortest path, whereas three or more force a strictly positive extra cost unless the endpoints differ only by rescaling. This gap persists with more prompts, small content perturbations, and aligned multihead outputs. On ViT-B/16 with VTAB-1k, a quadratic Bézier prompt path that minimizes feature action reduces held-out transport action relative to linear and cross-entropy-trained paths between the same endpoints and improves intermediate and online accuracy under unlabeled distribution shift, while cross-entropy-trained paths retain higher minimum accuracy when labeled calibration data are available. Reachable endpoints do not guarantee a reachable shortest path: how prompts move matters, not only where they end. Project page: this https URL.

[CV-60] Learning Anisotropic Prediction Targets for Flow Models

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17767

类目: Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)
备注: Project page: this https URL

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Flow-based generative models are trained on a hand-chosen prediction target, such as the velocity, the noise, or the clean data. This choice strongly affects training and sample quality. We introduce Anisotropic Flow Models (AnisoFlow), which replaces a fixed prediction target with a fully learnable one. AnisoFlow learns to scale the noise component of the target anisotropically, assigning a separate nonnegative weight to each direction in a jointly learned basis. A closed-form map recovers the velocity from the network’s prediction, leaving the architecture, flow-matching loss, and sampler unchanged. Since recovery is exact, training optimizes the target purely to make it easier for the network to predict. We theoretically analyze the resulting prediction error and predict that the preferred noise weight in each direction scales near-linearly with data variance, in agreement with the weights learned empirically in pixel space. AnisoFlow improves FID from 1.90 to 1.55 over the JiT baseline on ImageNet 256 \times 256, by learning only the prediction target, without representation alignment or architectural changes. In pixel-space text-to-image generation, AnisoFlow improves image quality and, through better-conditioned velocity recovery, largely removes patch-seam artifacts under reduced-precision inference.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Sparse-view 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) is prone to overfitting because only a limited number of images are available to constrain scene geometry. Dropout is one regularization strategy for alleviating this problem. We introduce the concept of a lineage family to describe a set of Gaussian primitives derived from the same founder through cloning or splitting operations. The founder Gaussians refer to all Gaussian primitives generated during initialization. Descendants within the same family often jointly contribute to the refinement of adjacent local regions. We further propose Lineage-Balanced Densification(LBD), which operates during densification by constructing an expanded candidate pool and reallocating the densification budget according to the mean gradient scores and existing descendant sizes of different families. Experiments on the LLFF, MipNeRF360, and Blender datasets show that the proposed framework achieves competitive performance in sparse-view reconstruction

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Medical visual question answering models can answer correctly without attending to the anatomy that the question names. We present QCAP-VQA, which supervises a subset of cross-attention heads with a question-conditioned prior built from ranked segmentation proposals. Its capped prior-mass objective penalizes insufficient attention inside the prior only below a threshold. We evaluate attention against SLAKE structure boxes that play no role in training, separating attention on the named anatomy from how attention shifts between questions about the same image. The cost lies in question conditioning: as the constraint widens or strengthens, attention shifts agree less with the expert-defined contrast. Across 13 configurations, prior-referenced and expert-referenced evaluation rank the models in largely opposite order (Spearman correlation of -0.87), and the capped models it favors track the prior’s own errors. Because clinical use of medical visual question answering requires answers grounded in the queried anatomy, supervision from automatic priors should be judged against independent anatomical references.

[CV-69] RaFL: An Empirical Study of Random Filter Losses for Image Reconstruction

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17707
作者: Ruibo Wang,Fengbei Liu,Aleksei Tiulpin,Mert R. Sabuncu,Heejong Kim
类目: Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)
备注:

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Pixel losses train image reconstruction networks toward a point statistic of the target distribution, often averaging away fine textures under uncertainty. Perceptual losses alleviate this issue using pretrained features, but those features are inherently domain-specific. We study a random filter loss (RaFL) that compares prediction and target through filters with random weights, avoiding both pretrained features and task-specific loss engineering. RaFL combines random convolutions, random Fourier features, and random wavelet filters, automatically balancing their contributions through gradient-norm matching, eliminating manual loss-weight tuning and grid search. We systematically investigate three design choices: filter family, weight resampling schedule, and weight initialization. Based on validation PSNR and stability across random seeds, we select the configuration that uses all three filter families, redraws filters every optimization step, and employs Gaussian initialization. We evaluate RaFL on three 2D and two 3D image reconstruction tasks. RaFL outperforms a pixel loss on three tasks and matches it on two, and demonstrates stable performance across random seeds on all five tasks. RaFL adds less than 2% to the backbone’s forward FLOPs and no inference cost, requiring 182\times fewer loss FLOPs than a VGG19 perceptual loss. These findings suggest that RaFL is a simple, tuning-free, and domain-agnostic loss objective for 2D and 3D image reconstruction.

[CV-71] Provable Cross-Modal Transfer of Hierarchical Concept Structure in CLIP Embeddings NEURIPS2026

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.17671
作者: Nghia Nguyen,Tianjiao Ding,Lachlan Ewen MacDonald,René Vidal
类目: Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)
备注: NeurIPS 2026

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Contrastive vision-language models such as CLIP are trained to align images and text, not to represent taxonomies. Yet empirical studies show that CLIP embeddings exhibit hierarchical structure aligned with the hierarchy of semantic concepts. A theoretical characterization of why such hierarchy emerges from a non-hierarchical objective remains open. In this paper, we formalize this phenomenon as Hierarchical Angular Separation (HAS), a geometric property whereby each non-leaf concept embedding is more similar to its descendants than to off-branch concepts. Empirically, we show that HAS holds approximately in raw images and randomly initialized CLIP image embeddings, while in text embeddings it holds approximately only after contrastive learning. To theoretically explain this asymmetry, we analyze an unconstrained features model in which the image embeddings are fixed and only the text embeddings are trained. At the global minimizer, the text-side HAS violation percentage at each node is at most the image-side HAS violation and near-violation percentage. The key mechanism is the hierarchical co-occurrence structure of image-text pairs, which biases text embeddings toward root-centered image prototypes. For a linear contrastive loss, we derive a closed-form solution that makes this mechanism explicit. We extend the analysis to the InfoNCE loss and establish analogous guarantees under sufficient regularization. Synthetic experiments validate the theory and show that HAS is transferred even beyond the regimes covered by our analysis.

We show that anatomically meaningful target selection is promising for retinal OCT representation learning, while greater anatomical specificity did not consistently improve performance.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Reward models are a core component of modern text-to-image systems, serving as the objective for inference-time selection and RL-based post-training. 点击查看摘要

Abstract:A rescue robot entering a building with thirty seconds on the clock should sprint toward the single most promising room; given ten minutes, it should instead explore a broader tour. Yet vision-language-navigation (VLN) models are typically evaluated under a fixed episode limit that the policy never observes, even though time-critical missions require behavior to adapt to the available budget. We study budget-conditioned object search: given a target category and an explicit action budget, an agent must find as many distinct target instances as possible before the budget expires. We investigate how to supervise VLNs for this task by comparing a privileged TSP expert, which plans near-optimal routes using the full map and true target locations, with a room-level Bellman expert that maximizes expected return under partial observability using high-level cross-scene semantic priors. Despite the TSP expert achieving substantially higher expert performance, its demonstrations are less effective for learning exploration: on held-out HM3D scenes, the Bellman-supervised VLN consistently outperforms the TSP-supervised VLN, with the advantage becoming more pronounced at longer budgets. At 2000 steps (500m), the Bellman-supervised VLN finds 71.4% of reachable target instances, compared with 65.5% for the TSP-supervised VLN and 44.8% for the pretrained VLN. These results highlight a distinction between optimal routing and optimal exploration: effective exploration supervision should optimize decisions under partial observability rather than imitate routes that are optimal only once the environment is fully known.

Recovered 3D sac surfaces have a median Chamfer distance of 0.34 mm on the internal cohort. X-Strata may therefore provide layered aneurysm and vessel information, including 3D sac shape, for patients who undergo only 2D angiography.

[AI-1] Alignment by Socialization: Learning Tradeoffs Under Selection

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18531
作者: Thomas Brennan,Andrew W. Lo,Chaoyi Zhao
类目: Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Populations and Evolution (q-bio.PE); General Finance (q-fin.GN)
备注:

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Artificial intelligence (AI) agents are expected to complete tasks while following rules of conduct, but these objectives can conflict. We study how evolutionary selection shapes an agent’s choices between them, and whether experience can make those choices reflect human judgments and withstand subsequent pressures. In our model, copies of an agent face a common uncertain reward state, and only those taking the rewarded action reproduce. We show that long-run growth is maximized when each action is taken as often as the environment rewards it. We compare three policies. A task-only policy thrives in a sandbox rewarding completion alone but fails when conduct matters. A fixed linear policy increasingly favors task completion as its stakes rise, even where humans never reward it. A learned policy instead converges to the tradeoffs rewarded by its environment; when humans supply the rewards, we call this socialization. Accumulated experience makes socialization harder to reverse under subsequent task-only training, although violations can arise before the prevailing disposition changes, and finite memory weakens this protection. When a single violation can end a deployment, a hard prohibition can prevent it, but a mistaken prohibition can also be costly. Experiments with small neural networks illustrate how training targets and memory shape these outcomes. Our results suggest that alignment depends not only on the objectives assigned to agents, but also on the environments that shape and sustain their behavior.

[AI-3] yping Matters: Muscle Language and Typing Behaviour in Live Keystroke Decoding from sEMG

类目: Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)
备注:

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Surface electromyography (sEMG) from the wrists can recover typed text without a keyboard. It is usually posed as a signal-inference problem: characters are read out of the muscle signal and character error rate is reported offline. We argue that this framing removes most of the problem. The decoder is constrained simultaneously by the muscles, by the language being typed, and by typing behaviour such as typos, corrections, backspaces and line breaks, and a usable system must predict keys live while the hand keeps moving. Existing systems fall short on two counts: (1) they process sEMG in fixed windows and carry nothing from one window to the next, discarding context accumulated over a session, and (2) they lack any understanding of the linguistic structure that drives typing, so every keystroke is inferred from the muscle signal alone. We propose a fully causal, zero-lookahead sEMG typing decoder built on a state space model (SSM), in which a single model decodes a whole session without windowed partitions, conditioned on what has already been typed. We achieve this by fusing a traditional typing decoder with an efficient autoregressive language model, instilling both sEMG and linguistic understanding in one model. To train such a model for per-character typing rather than whole-word text, we introduce a retokenization method that rewrites ordinary prose as human-like typing, with keystrokes, typos, backspaces and line breaks included, converting WikiText into a large typing corpus where none existed. Our model, having never seen a single sEMG-to-character transcript in typing, achieves a state-of-the-art character error rate of 25.13% on emg2qwerty’s cross-user benchmark, a 5.4% relative improvement over the previous best with 6.7 times fewer parameters, while running strictly causally with no future context.

[AI-5] Black-Box Uncertainty Quantification for Large Language Models via Ensemble-of-Ensembles NEURIPS2026

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18453
作者: Wang Ma,Debarun Bhattacharjya,Junkyu Lee,Nhan H Pham,Harsha Kokel,Qiang Ji
类目: Machine Learning (cs.LG); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)
备注: Accepted at NeurIPS 2026. 54 pages (10 pages main text), 3 figures, 38 tables. Code: this https URL

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Reliable use of large language models (LLMs) requires uncertainty estimates that separate ambiguity intrinsic to a prompt from genuine knowledge gaps. Bayesian and deep-ensemble methods deliver this aleatoric/epistemic decomposition in principle, but are computationally prohibitive at LLM scale and require white-box access. Existing black-box methods, in contrast, summarize to a single scalar that cannot make the distinction. We introduce a two-level ensemble that bridges this gap. Stochastic decoding of a fixed prompt forms the inner ensemble; meaning-preserving semantic perturbations of the prompt form the outer ensemble. Variability is measured in a continuous embedding space, and the law of total covariance gives an exact within-/between-perturbation decomposition to estimate aleatoric and epistemic uncertainty (AU/EU); it requires only black-box sampling access. On top of this estimator, we contribute three theoretical results that ground the framework: (i) a finite-sample bias identity for nested Monte Carlo with a closed-form bias-corrected epistemic estimator; (ii) a Bayesian bridge bounding the gap between perturbation-population AU/EU and Bayesian targets; and (iii) a separation result identifying a class of failure modes that any consistency-only black-box method provably misses while perturbation-based EU detects. Across five short QA benchmarks and four instruction-tuned LLMs spanning two families and two scales, our estimator is competitive with strong white-box baselines, and at a matched generation budget it matches leading black-box methods on error ranking while decomposing AU and EU; controlled paired interventions show that AU and EU respond selectively to distinct, actionable sources of failure: prompt ambiguity and model knowledge gaps; and a long-form pilot on the NQ long-answer split shows the framework extends to claim-level factuality without retraining.

Abstract:Humans know that airplanes are fast, that this extends to red airplanes, but that it does not hold for broken airplanes. We first assess open-source pretrained models using 199 curated category-feature pairs and analyze their training data. Our findings suggest that strengthening context and category-feature co-occurrence likelihood (PMI) improve learning for larger models and verbatim rule-like statements diminish flexible category learning. To systematically isolate these effects, we then train a 68M model from scratch with precise control over the categorical information each sees in their training data, confirming that verbatim repetition reduces flexible learning but mixture of diverse knowledge expressions lead to more robust category learning. Together, our findings highlight that while contextual flexibility is persistently difficult for models, how category knowledge is expressed in pretraining greatly impacts models’ acquisition of robust category knowledge.

[AI-8] VelServe: Taming Decode Jitters in LLM Inference with Tail-Calibrated Scheduling

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18392
作者: Ishaan Datta,Alind Khare,Koushik Kumar Dey,Ratnesh Jamidar,Anand Kumar Singh
类目: Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)
备注:

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Interactive LLM services must meet latency service-level objectives (SLOs) for the time to first token (TTFT) and the time between tokens (TBT), as streaming and soft real-time applications require predictable token delivery. We identify decode jitters in both aggregated and disaggregated serving. A jitter delays every request in the batch, and long responses span hundreds of steps, so even rare overruns cause many SLO violations. In aggregated serving, SLO-aware schedulers set each step’s prefill budget until its predicted latency just meets the TBT target, leaving no room for mispredictions or host stalls. Disaggregation meets average TBT targets at a cost in capacity, yet the steps that admit new requests still run late. We present \sysname, a scheduler that calibrates how much prefill work each decode step carries using the measured tail of its own iteration latency. The SLO determines how rare a late step must be, and \sysname leaves enough headroom in each iteration to prevent overruns and meet the target decode latency. This headroom slows prefill, so \sysname serves the shortest waiting prompt first. When TTFT deadlines grow with prompt length, the long prompts it delays have the most time to spare. Under disaggregation, it spaces admissions so that no request waits on more slow admission steps than its P99 target allows. On three attention families and varying workloads, \sysname sustains 2.4–2.9 \times the SLO goodput of the strongest baseline on Qwen3-8B and DeepSeek-V4-Flash, and at least 4.4 \times on DeepSeek-V2-Lite. With prefill and decode disaggregated on eight GPUs, \sysname meets the SLO for more than twice as many requests as state-of-the-art baselines. [AI-9] Vision-Language-Action Models: A Life-Cycle Survey of Design Training and Deployment

链接: https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.18372
作者: Suvarna Kadam(Witsense AI),Soran Parsa(University of Huddersfield)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Vision-language-action (VLA) models adapt web-pretrained vision-language models to output robot actions, so that web knowledge carries over to physical control. In under 4 years the field has moved from RT-2, the first VLA, to open models that fine-tune on a single consumer GPU and to proprietary generalists that clean unseen homes. This survey reviews 88 works from December 2022 to October 2026, mainly on manipulation and navigation, plus humanoid control, world-action models and evaluation. We follow a VLA through its life cycle, from model design to training and deployment, along seven axes. Model design covers (a) inputs and memory, what the model observes; (b) architecture, how its pipeline is built; © action output, how it produces motor commands; and (d) intermediate predictions, what else it predicts, from reasoning steps to future observations. Training covers (e) pretraining data, the mix of robot, web, human and simulated data, and (f) post-training, adaptation to the target robot. Deployment covers (g) real-time execution, keeping pace with the robot. We analyse 22 models on these axes and report their training data, model size, inference speed and hardware. Three trends emerge: (i) training data has broadened, from about 10,000 hours of teleoperated robot data ( \pi_0 ) to 23,000 community-collected episodes (SmolVLA) and 20,000 hours of egocentric human video (GR00T N1.7); (ii) model sizes have split: most recent models have 3-7 billion parameters, while a new group under one billion runs on consumer GPUs; and (iii) generalist VLAs are emerging that handle both manipulation and navigation, such as UniVLA and Qwen-VLA. We also compare VLAs with world-action models, which predict future observations as well as actions. We review evidence that some VLAs with near-perfect benchmark scores fail when objects or the camera move slightly, and open up further research questions.

[AI-10] Causal AI-Driven Secure mmWave Beam Alignment with Defensive Variational Autoencoders

备注:

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Efficient and secure beam alignment is essential for mmWave communication systems. However, existing deep learning (DL)-based beam alignment methods often neglect causal relationships between inputs and outputs, resulting in limited interpretability, poor generalization, and excessive beam sweeping. Moreover, these DL solutions are vulnerable to adversarial attacks, where small perturbations in received power measurements can mislead beam classifiers. To overcome these issues, we propose a causally-aware DL framework integrating causal discovery into beam alignment. A two-stage causal beam selection algorithm learns a Bayesian graph capturing dependencies between received signal strength indicators (RSSI) as inputs and the optimal beam index as the target variable, then uses it to select a minimal set of relevant input features for the DL classifier. To mitigate adversarial attacks, we introduce a defensive variational autoencoder (DVAE) that maps potentially perturbed inputs back to their clean versions. End-to-end fine-tuning of the downstream beam classifier on the DVAE-reconstructed RSSI representations further boosts prediction accuracy. Simulation results show that causal beam selection achieves accuracy comparable to conventional methods while providing an (\mathbf829\times) lower per-sample computational cost than SHAP (Shapley Additive Explanations), a widely used explainable AI (XAI)-based feature selection method. It further reduces beam-sweeping overhead by (\mathbf59.4%) and (\mathbf89.8%), compared to conventional DL-based beam selection and exhaustive search, respectively. The DVAE-based defense effectively denoises adversarial inputs, sustaining 85% accuracy across different perturbation bounds, retains baseline performance on unperturbed RSSI inputs and surpasses defensive distillation defense scheme by 15–20% on average under different attack scenarios.
